export const metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'About',
  description:
    'Why Nyxora Sync exists: a free, complete, written curriculum for web development and programming languages, from first program to production mastery.',
  path: '/about',
  keywords: ['about Nyxora Sync', 'free programming curriculum', 'open source coding education'],
})

const moduleKeys = new Set<string>()
const lessonIds = new Set<string>()
for (const track of tracks) {
  for (const mod of track.modules) {
    moduleKeys.add(mod.key)
    for (const lesson of mod.lessons) lessonIds.add(lesson.id)
  }
}

const stats = [
  { value: `${tracks.length}`, label: 'guided paths' },
  { value: `${moduleKeys.size}`, label: 'modules' },
  { value: `${lessonIds.size}`, label: 'written lessons' },
  { value: '$0', label: 'free forever' },
]

===== FILE: src/app/accessibility/page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import { LegalPage } from '@/components/legal-page'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

export const metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Accessibility',
  description:
    'Nyxora Sync aims for WCAG 2.2 AA. What works today, the known gaps, and how to report an accessibility barrier.',
  path: '/accessibility',
})

export default function AccessibilityPage() {
  return (
    <LegalPage
      badge="Accessibility"
      title="Accessibility"
      intro="A free curriculum is only free if the people who need it can use it. This page records where we are, what is still broken, and how to tell us."
      updated="October 20, 2026"
    >
      <h2>Our target</h2>
      <p>
        Nyxora Sync aims to meet the{' '}
        <a href="https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
          Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) 2.2, Level AA
        </a>
        . This is a target, not a certification. The site has not yet been independently audited.
      </p>

      <h2>What is in place</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>Semantic headings, landmarks, lists, and form labels; a visible focus outline on interactive elements.</li>
        <li>Keyboard-operable navigation, dialogs, menus, filters, and forms, including Escape to close dialogs.</li>
        <li>Text alternatives for meaningful icons and empty alt text for decorative images.</li>
        <li>Theme-aware color contrast designed against the WCAG AA thresholds for normal and large text.</li>
        <li>Layouts that reflow at 200% zoom and on narrow screens without requiring horizontal scrolling for prose.</li>
        <li>No autoplaying audio or video. The homepage has a slow, low-contrast ambient visual that becomes a still image when reduced motion is requested.</li>
        <li>Reduced-motion support: page transitions, reveal effects, and the homepage visual are removed or made static when your system asks for less motion.</li>
        <li>Code blocks that remain readable and copyable without a mouse.</li>
        <li>Forms that report errors in text, not color alone, and keep their labels associated with inputs.</li>
      </ul>

      <h2>Known limitations</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>
          <strong>No independent audit yet.</strong> The checks above are part of development, not a
          formal conformance test with assistive technology.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>The code playground.</strong> The live preview runs inside a sandboxed frame and the
          editor is a plain text area. Some screen-reader and mobile keyboard combinations may still
          be awkward; the lesson text and code samples are complete without the playground.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>User-generated images.</strong> Project screenshots are treated as decorative. A
          project owner can describe the image in the project description, but there is no separate
          alt-text field yet.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Third-party resources.</strong> Linked videos, articles, and documentation are
          outside our control and may not meet the same standard.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Older lessons.</strong> A few diagrams or long code samples may need better structure
          on small screens. They are being fixed as they are reviewed.
        </li>
      </ul>

      <h2>How we test</h2>
      <p>
        Accessibility is checked during development with keyboard-only navigation, browser zoom,
        automated linting, and contrast review of the theme palette. When a barrier is reported, we
        reproduce it, fix it, and add the case to the checks for future changes.
      </p>

      <h2>Compatibility</h2>
      <p>
        The site is tested in current versions of Chrome, Firefox, Safari, and Edge on desktop, and in
        mobile Safari and Chrome on a small viewport. It does not require JavaScript for reading
        lessons, but interactive progress, the playground, and community features do.
      </p>

      <h2>Report a barrier</h2>
      <p>
        If something blocks you, use the <Link href="/contact">contact form</Link> and choose
        &ldquo;General question&rdquo; or &ldquo;Bug or broken page&rdquo;. Describe the page, what
        you were trying to do, the assistive technology or input method you use, and what happened.
        You can also open a GitHub issue labeled <code>accessibility</code>.
      </p>
      <p>
        We will acknowledge the report, tell you whether we can reproduce it, and explain what we plan
        to do. Reports about a lesson&apos;s code or explanation are handled like any other content
        correction.
      </p>

      <h2>Feedback on this statement</h2>
      <p>
        This statement is reviewed when the site changes substantially. If something here is
        inaccurate, say so through the contact form; an honest statement is more useful than an
        optimistic one.
      </p>
    </LegalPage>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/admin/messages/page.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import { CheckCircle2, Clock, Inbox, Mail, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { ContactMessageActions } from '@/components/admin/contact-message-actions'
import { auth } from '@/lib/auth/auth'
import { isAdmin } from '@/lib/admin'
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Contact inbox',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
}

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

const PAGE_SIZE = 20

type InboxFilter = 'all' | 'open' | 'handled'

function firstParam(value: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value
}

function readableDate(date: Date): string {
  return `${date.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 16)} UTC`
}

function pageHref(page: number, filter: InboxFilter): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams()
  if (filter !== 'all') params.set('filter', filter)
  if (page > 1) params.set('page', String(page))
  const query = params.toString()
  return query ? `/admin/messages?${query}` : '/admin/messages'
}

export default async function ContactInboxPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ page?: string | string[]; filter?: string | string[] }>
}) {
  const session = await auth()
  if (!isAdmin(session?.user?.email)) notFound()
  if (!process.env.DATABASE_URL) notFound()

  const params = await searchParams
  const filterParam = firstParam(params.filter)
  const filter: InboxFilter =
    filterParam === 'open' || filterParam === 'handled' ? filterParam : 'all'
  const requestedPage = Number.parseInt(firstParam(params.page) ?? '1', 10)
  const page = Number.isFinite(requestedPage) && requestedPage > 0 ? requestedPage : 1

  const where =
    filter === 'open'
      ? { handled: false }
      : filter === 'handled'
        ? { handled: true }
        : {}

  const [total, openCount] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.contactMessage.count({ where }),
    prisma.contactMessage.count({ where: { handled: false } }),
  ])

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE))
  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages)

  const messages = await prisma.contactMessage.findMany({
    where,
    orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
    skip: (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE,
    take: PAGE_SIZE,
  })

  const emailNotifications = Boolean(process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_HOST)

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 py-10 sm:py-14 pb-20 sm:pb-0">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-8 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1.5">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              Maintainer only
            </Badge>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">Contact inbox</h1>
            <p className="max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">
              Private messages sent through the contact form. Reply by email, then mark the message
              handled so it disappears from the open queue.
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Signed in as {session?.user?.email} · {openCount} unhandled · {total}{' '}
              {filter === 'all' ? 'total' : filter === 'open' ? 'open' : 'handled'}
            </p>
          </div>

          {!emailNotifications && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
              <Mail className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-medium">Email notifications are not configured</p>
                <p className="mt-1">
                  Messages are still stored safely. Check this inbox instead of relying on email.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <Button asChild size="sm" variant={filter === 'all' ? 'default' : 'outline'}>
                <Link href={pageHref(1, 'all')}>All messages</Link>
              </Button>
              <Button asChild size="sm" variant={filter === 'open' ? 'default' : 'outline'}>
                <Link href={pageHref(1, 'open')}>Open {openCount > 0 ? `(${openCount})` : ''}</Link>
              </Button>
              <Button asChild size="sm" variant={filter === 'handled' ? 'default' : 'outline'}>
                <Link href={pageHref(1, 'handled')}>Handled</Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
            <Badge variant="secondary" className="gap-1.5">
              <Inbox className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              {total} in this view
            </Badge>
          </div>

          {messages.length === 0 ? (
            <Card>
              <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-14 text-center">
                <Inbox className="h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground/50" aria-hidden="true" />
                <div>
                  <p className="font-medium">No messages here</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    {filter === 'open'
                      ? 'Everything is handled. Nice.'
                      : 'Messages sent through the contact form will appear here.'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {messages.map((message) => (
                <Card key={message.id} className={message.handled ? 'opacity-75' : undefined}>
                  <CardContent className="pt-5">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                          <h2 className="font-semibold">{message.name?.trim() || 'Anonymous'}</h2>
                          <Badge variant="outline" className="capitalize">
                            {message.topic}
                          </Badge>
                          {message.handled ? (
                            <Badge variant="secondary" className="gap-1">
                              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                              Handled
                            </Badge>
                          ) : (
                            <Badge variant="default" className="gap-1">
                              <Clock className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                              Open
                            </Badge>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {readableDate(message.createdAt)} · {message.email}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-2">
                        <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-2">
                          <a
                            href={`mailto:${message.email}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(
                              `Re: [Nyxora Sync] ${message.topic} message`
                            )}`}
                          >
                            <Mail className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                            Reply
                          </a>
                        </Button>
                        <ContactMessageActions id={message.id} handled={message.handled} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-relaxed">
                      {message.message}
                    </p>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              {currentPage > 1 ? (
                <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
                  <Link href={pageHref(currentPage - 1, filter)}>Previous</Link>
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled>
                  Previous
                </Button>
              )}
              <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
              </span>
              {currentPage < totalPages ? (
                <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
                  <Link href={pageHref(currentPage + 1, filter)}>Next</Link>
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled>
                  Next
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/app/admin/newsletter/page.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import { Mail, ShieldCheck, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { NewsletterAdminForm } from '@/components/admin/newsletter-admin-form'
import { auth } from '@/lib/auth/auth'
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'
import { isNewsletterAdmin, newsletterEmailConfigured } from '@/lib/newsletter'
import { NEWSLETTER_ENABLED } from '@/lib/newsletter-feature'

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Newsletter admin',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
}

// Authentication and subscriber counts are request-specific.

function readableDate(date: Date): string {
  return `${date.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 16)} UTC`
}

export default async function NewsletterAdminPage() {
  if (!NEWSLETTER_ENABLED) notFound()

  const session = await auth()
  if (!isNewsletterAdmin(session?.user?.email)) notFound()
  if (!process.env.DATABASE_URL) notFound()

  const [confirmedCount, pendingCount, recentSends] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.newsletterSubscriber.count({ where: { confirmedAt: { not: null } } }),
    prisma.newsletterSubscriber.count({ where: { confirmedAt: null } }),
    prisma.newsletterSend.findMany({
      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
      take: 8,
    }),
  ])

  const emailReady = newsletterEmailConfigured()

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 py-10 sm:py-14 pb-20 sm:pb-0">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-8 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1.5">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              Maintainer only
            </Badge>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">Newsletter admin</h1>
            <p className="max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">
              Write one plain-text email and send it to confirmed subscribers. Every email includes
              a signed unsubscribe link. Nothing is scheduled or retried automatically.
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Signed in as {session?.user?.email} · {confirmedCount} confirmed · {pendingCount}{' '}
              unconfirmed
            </p>
          </div>

          {!emailReady && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
              <TriangleAlert className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-medium">Email delivery is not configured</p>
                <p className="mt-1">
                  Set <code>EMAIL_SERVER_HOST</code>, <code>EMAIL_SERVER_PORT</code>, and{' '}
                  <code>EMAIL_FROM</code> before sending. Newsletter signup will stay unavailable
                  until then.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <Card>
            <CardContent className="pt-6">
              <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Mail className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Compose</h2>
              </div>
              <NewsletterAdminForm
                adminEmail={session!.user.email!}
                confirmedCount={confirmedCount}
              />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardContent className="pt-6">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Recent sends</h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Metadata only. The message body is not stored. Test sends are included.
              </p>
              {recentSends.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border/70 px-3 py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  No newsletter emails have been sent yet.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-4 overflow-x-auto">
                  <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
                    <thead className="border-b border-border/60 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
                      <tr>
                        <th className="px-2 py-2 font-medium">Subject</th>
                        <th className="px-2 py-2 font-medium">Type</th>
                        <th className="px-2 py-2 font-medium">Recipients</th>
                        <th className="px-2 py-2 font-medium">Failed</th>
                        <th className="px-2 py-2 font-medium">Date</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {recentSends.map((send) => (
                        <tr key={send.id} className="border-b border-border/40 last:border-0">
                          <td className="max-w-xs truncate px-2 py-3">{send.subject}</td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-3">
                            <Badge variant={send.isTest ? 'secondary' : 'outline'}>
                              {send.isTest ? 'Test' : 'Broadcast'}
                            </Badge>
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-3">{send.recipientCount}</td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-3">{send.failedCount}</td>
                          <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-2 py-3 text-muted-foreground">
                            {readableDate(send.createdAt)}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card className="border-dashed">
            <CardContent className="pt-6">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">How the list works</h2>
              <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                <li>Signups are unconfirmed until the address opens its confirmation email.</li>
                <li>Only confirmed addresses are included in a broadcast.</li>
                <li>
                  Unsubscribe links are signed, work from old emails, and delete the address
                  immediately.
                </li>
                <li>
                  Each real send is limited to 500 recipients and one broadcast per minute to avoid
                  accidental duplicate sends.
                </li>
              </ul>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/auth/error/page.tsx =====
'use client'

import { Suspense } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { AlertCircle, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'

const errorMessages: Record<string, { title: string; description: string }> = {
  Configuration: {
    title: 'Configuration Error',
    description: 'Authentication is not properly configured. Please contact support or try again later.',
  },
  AccessDenied: {
    title: 'Access Denied',
    description: 'You do not have permission to sign in. Please check your credentials or contact support.',
  },
  Verification: {
    title: 'Verification Failed',
    description: 'The sign-in link has expired or has already been used. Please request a new one.',
  },
  OAuthSignin: {
    title: 'OAuth Error',
    description: 'An error occurred during OAuth sign-in. Please try again or use a different provider.',
  },
  OAuthCallback: {
    title: 'OAuth Callback Error',
    description: 'An error occurred during the OAuth callback. Please try again.',
  },
  OAuthCreateAccount: {
    title: 'Account Creation Error',
    description: 'Could not create account. An account with this email may already exist.',
  },
  EmailCreateAccount: {
    title: 'Account Creation Error',
    description: 'Could not create account. Please try signing in with a different method.',
  },
  Callback: {
    title: 'Callback Error',
    description: 'An error occurred during the authentication callback. Please try again.',
  },
  EmailSignin: {
    title: 'Email Sign In Error',
    description: 'Failed to send sign-in email. Please check your email address and try again.',
  },
  CredentialsSignin: {
    title: 'Invalid Credentials',
    description: 'The provided credentials are invalid. Please check and try again.',
  },
  Default: {
    title: 'Authentication Error',
    description: 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again or contact support.',
  },
}

export default function AuthErrorPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground">Loading…</div>}>
      <AuthErrorContent />
    </Suspense>
  )
}

function AuthErrorContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const error = searchParams.get('error') || 'Default'
  const errorInfo = errorMessages[error] || errorMessages.Default

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 pb-20 sm:pb-0">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md text-center">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <div className="mx-auto mb-4 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-destructive/10">
                <AlertCircle className="h-7 w-7 text-destructive" aria-hidden="true" />
              </div>
              <CardTitle>{errorInfo.title}</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <p className="text-muted-foreground">{errorInfo.description}</p>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
                <Button onClick={() => window.history.back()}>
                  <RefreshCw className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Try Again
                </Button>
                <Button variant="outline" asChild>
                  <Link href="/auth/signin">Back to Sign In</Link>
                </Button>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
          <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Need help? <Link href="/contact" className="underline hover:text-foreground">Contact support</Link>
          </p>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/auth/signin/page.tsx =====
import { SignInClient } from './signin-client'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

export const metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Sign In',
  description: 'Sign in to Nyxora Sync to sync your progress across devices.',
  path: '/auth/signin',
  noIndex: true,
})

// Force dynamic rendering so DATABASE_URL is read at request time (available in Vercel runtime)
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export default function SignInPage() {
  const enabled = {
    github: Boolean(process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID && process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET),
    google: Boolean(process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID && process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET),
    email: Boolean(process.env.EMAIL_SERVER),
  }
  const hasDatabase = Boolean(process.env.DATABASE_URL)

  return <SignInClient enabled={enabled} hasDatabase={hasDatabase} />
}

===== FILE: src/app/auth/signin/signin-client.tsx =====
'use client'

import { Suspense, useState } from 'react'
import { signIn } from 'next-auth/react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import Image from 'next/image'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import { Loader2, BookOpen, ShieldCheck, Mail, Globe, UserCog } from 'lucide-react'
import { GithubIcon } from '@/components/icons/brand'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'

export type EnabledProviders = {
  github: boolean
  google: boolean
  email: boolean
}

const oauthProviders = [
  { id: 'github', name: 'GitHub', icon: GithubIcon },
  { id: 'google', name: 'Google', icon: Globe },
]

export function SignInClient({ enabled, hasDatabase }: { enabled: EnabledProviders; hasDatabase: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<SignInFallback />}>
      <SignInContent enabled={enabled} hasDatabase={hasDatabase} />
    </Suspense>
  )
}

function SignInFallback() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 pb-20 sm:pb-0">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
          <div className="h-96 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

function SignInContent({ enabled, hasDatabase }: { enabled: EnabledProviders; hasDatabase: boolean }) {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const callbackUrl = searchParams.get('callbackUrl') || '/learn'
  const error = searchParams.get('error')
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [adminUsername, setAdminUsername] = useState('')
  const [adminPassword, setAdminPassword] = useState('')
  const [adminError, setAdminError] = useState('')
  const [showAdminForm, setShowAdminForm] = useState(false)

  const availableOAuth = oauthProviders.filter(
    (p) => enabled[p.id as 'github' | 'google']
  )
  const availableAdmin = hasDatabase
  const hasAnyProvider = availableOAuth.length > 0 || enabled.email || availableAdmin
  const singleOAuthOnly = availableOAuth.length === 1 && !enabled.email && !availableAdmin
  const singleProviderName = availableOAuth[0]?.name ?? 'GitHub'

  const handleOAuthSignIn = (provider: string) => {
    setIsLoading(provider)
    signIn(provider, { callbackUrl })
  }

  const handleAdminSignIn = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!adminUsername || !adminPassword) return

    setIsLoading('admin')
    setAdminError('')

    try {
      const result = await signIn('admin', {
        username: adminUsername,
        password: adminPassword,
        callbackUrl,
        redirect: false,
      })

      if (result?.error) {
        setAdminError('Invalid credentials. Please try again.')
        setIsLoading(null)
        return
      }

      window.location.assign(result?.url || callbackUrl)
    } catch {
      setAdminError('Sign in is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.')
      setIsLoading(null)
    }
  }

  const handleEmailSignIn = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!email) return
    setIsLoading('email')
    await signIn('email', { email, callbackUrl })
  }

  const renderProviders = () => {
    if (showAdminForm) {
      return (
        <form onSubmit={handleAdminSignIn} className="space-y-4">
          {adminError && (
            <p role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-destructive">
              {adminError}
            </p>
          )}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="username">Admin email</Label>
            <Input
              id="username"
              type="text"
              placeholder="admin@nyxora.local"
              value={adminUsername}
              onChange={(e) => setAdminUsername(e.target.value)}
              disabled={isLoading !== null}
              required
              autoComplete="username"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={adminPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setAdminPassword(e.target.value)}
              disabled={isLoading !== null}
              required
              autoComplete="current-password"
            />
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full gap-2" disabled={isLoading !== null || !adminUsername || !adminPassword}>
            {isLoading === 'admin' ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
                Signing in...
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <UserCog className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                Admin Sign In
              </>
            )}
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="ghost"
            className="w-full"
            onClick={() => setShowAdminForm(false)}
            disabled={isLoading !== null}
          >
            Back to options
          </Button>
        </form>
      )
    }

    return (
      <>
        {availableOAuth.length > 0 && (
          <div className={availableOAuth.length > 1 ? 'grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2' : 'mx-auto w-full max-w-xs'}>
            {availableOAuth.map((provider) => (
              <Button
                key={provider.id}
                variant="outline"
                className={availableOAuth.length === 1 ? 'w-full gap-2' : 'gap-2'}
                onClick={() => handleOAuthSignIn(provider.id)}
                disabled={isLoading !== null}
              >
                {isLoading === provider.id ? (
                  <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : (
                  <provider.icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                )}
                {provider.name}
              </Button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {availableAdmin && (
          <>
            {(availableOAuth.length > 0 || enabled.email) && <Separator className="my-4" />}
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              className="w-full max-w-xs mx-auto gap-2"
              onClick={() => setShowAdminForm(true)}
              disabled={isLoading !== null}
            >
              <UserCog className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              Admin Login
            </Button>
          </>
        )}

        {enabled.email && (
          <>
            {(availableOAuth.length > 0 || availableAdmin) && <Separator className="my-4" />}
            <form onSubmit={handleEmailSignIn} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="email">Email address</Label>
                <Input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isLoading !== null}
                  required
                  autoComplete="email"
                />
              </div>
              <Button type="submit" className="w-full gap-2" disabled={isLoading !== null || !email}>
                {isLoading === 'email' ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" />
                    Sending...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Mail className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    Send Magic Link
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </form>
          </>
        )}
      </>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 sm:py-20 pb-20 sm:pb-0">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
          <div className="text-center mb-8">
            <Link href="/" className="mb-6 inline-block">
              <Image
                src="/site-logo.jpg"
                alt="Nyxora Sync"
                width={480}
                height={240}
                sizes="260px"
                className="h-auto w-[260px] rounded-xl"
                priority
              />
            </Link>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Welcome back</h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">Sign in to sync progress across devices and join the community</p>
          </div>

          <Card>
            <CardHeader className="text-center">
              <CardTitle>
                {singleOAuthOnly
                  ? `Continue with ${singleProviderName}`
                  : hasAnyProvider
                    ? 'Choose a sign-in method'
                    : 'Accounts coming soon'}
              </CardTitle>
              <CardDescription>
                {singleOAuthOnly
                  ? `Use your ${singleProviderName} account to sync progress across devices.`
                  : hasAnyProvider
                    ? enabled.email
                      ? 'All free. No passwords. Magic links or OAuth.'
                      : availableAdmin
                        ? 'All free. No passwords. Use your GitHub, Google, or Admin account.'
                        : `All free. No passwords. Use your ${availableOAuth.map(p => p.name).join(' or ')} account.`
                    : 'No sign-in provider is configured on this deployment yet.'}
              </CardDescription>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              {error && (
                <div className="rounded-md bg-destructive/10 p-3 text-sm text-destructive" role="alert">
                  {error === 'Configuration' ? 'Authentication is not configured on this deployment.' : 'Something went wrong. Please try again.'}
                </div>
              )}

              {!hasAnyProvider && (
                <div className="rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/5 p-4 space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium text-sm">
                    <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                    You don&apos;t need an account to learn
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    All lessons, resources, and the playground are free and open. Your progress,
                    XP, streaks, and notes are saved automatically in this browser.
                  </p>
                  <Button asChild className="w-full gap-2">
                    <Link href="/learn">
                      Start learning now
                    </Link>
                  </Button>
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    No account, no tracking, no paywall.
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {renderProviders()}

              {hasAnyProvider && (
                <p className="text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  By continuing, you agree to our{' '}
                  <Link href="/terms" className="underline hover:text-foreground">Terms of Service</Link>{' '}
                  and{' '}
                  <Link href="/privacy" className="underline hover:text-foreground">Privacy Policy</Link>
                </p>
              )}
            </CardContent>
            <CardFooter className="flex justify-center border-t-0 pt-0">
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                New here?{' '}
                <Link href="/learn" className="font-medium text-foreground underline hover:text-primary">
                  Explore without signing in
                </Link>
              </p>
            </CardFooter>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/auth/verify-request/page.tsx =====
'use client'

import { Suspense } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Mail, CheckCircle, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

export default function VerifyRequestPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground">Loading…</div>}>
      <VerifyRequestContent />
    </Suspense>
  )
}

function VerifyRequestContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const type = searchParams.get('type') || 'email'
  const error = searchParams.get('error')

  const isError = !!error

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 pb-20 sm:pb-0">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md text-center">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <div className={cn(
                'mx-auto mb-4 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full',
                isError ? 'bg-destructive/10 text-destructive' : 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400'
              )}>
                {isError ? (
                  <AlertCircle className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : type === 'email' ? (
                  <Mail className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : (
                  <CheckCircle className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
                )}
              </div>
              <CardTitle>
                {isError ? 'Something went wrong' : 'Check your email'}
              </CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              {isError ? (
                <>
                  <p className="text-destructive">
                    {error === 'Config' ? 'Email not configured. Try GitHub or Google sign-in.' : 'Failed to send email. Please try again.'}
                  </p>
                  <Button variant="outline" asChild>
                    <Link href="/auth/signin">Back to Sign In</Link>
                  </Button>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground">
                    We&apos;ve sent a magic link to your email. The link expires in 24 hours.
                  </p>
                  <div className="rounded-lg bg-muted p-4 text-left text-sm">
                    <p className="font-medium mb-2">What happens next:</p>
                    <ul className="space-y-1 text-muted-foreground">
                      <li className="flex items-center gap-2"><CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />Open your email inbox</li>
                      <li className="flex items-center gap-2"><CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />Find the email from Nyxora Sync</li>
                      <li className="flex items-center gap-2"><CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />Click the sign-in link</li>
                      <li className="flex items-center gap-2"><CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />Start learning!</li>
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    Didn&apos;t receive it? Check spam folder or{' '}
                    <Link href="/auth/signin" className="underline hover:text-foreground">request another</Link>
                  </p>
                </>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
          <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            <Link href="/" className="underline hover:text-foreground">← Back to home</Link>
          </p>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/categories/[category]/page.tsx =====
import { Metadata } from 'next'
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { ArrowRight, Code2 as Code2Icon, Globe, Cpu, Smartphone, Brain, Cloud, Gamepad2, Monitor, Code2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { trackCategories, tracks as curriculumTracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { cn, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

interface CategoryPageProps {
  params: Promise<{ category: string }>
}

const categoryIcons: Record<string, typeof Globe> = {
  'web-development': Globe,
  'programming-languages': Code2,
  'embedded-systems': Cpu,
  'mobile-development': Smartphone,
  'data-science': Brain,
  'devops-cloud': Cloud,
  'game-development': Gamepad2,
  'desktop-apps': Monitor,
}

const trackIcons: Record<string, typeof Globe> = {
  beginner: Globe,
  developer: Code2,
  specializations: Globe,
  c: Globe,
  cpp: Code2,
  csharp: Code2,
  python: Globe,
  embedded: Cpu,
}

export async function generateStaticParams() {
  return trackCategories
    .filter((cat) => cat.id !== 'embedded-systems' && curriculumTracks.some((t) => t.category === cat.id))
    .map((cat) => ({ category: cat.id }))
}

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: CategoryPageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { category } = await params
  const catInfo = trackCategories.find((c) => c.id === category)
  if (!catInfo) return { title: 'Category Not Found' }
  
  const categoryTracks = curriculumTracks.filter((t) => t.category === category)
  const totalLessons = categoryTracks.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.modules.reduce((s, m) => s + m.lessons.length, 0), 0)
  
  return pageMetadata({
    title: `${catInfo.name} | Nyxora Sync`,
    description: `${catInfo.description} ${totalLessons} lessons across ${categoryTracks.length} learning paths.`,
    path: `/categories/${category}`,
  })
}

export default async function CategoryPage({ params }: CategoryPageProps) {
  const { category } = await params
  const catInfo = trackCategories.find((c) => c.id === category)
  
  if (!catInfo) notFound()
  
  const categoryTracks = curriculumTracks.filter((t) => t.category === category)
  
  if (categoryTracks.length === 0) notFound()
  
  const tracks = categoryTracks.map((track) => {
    const lessons = track.modules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.lessons.length, 0)
    const totalHours = track.modules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.estimatedHours, 0)
    return {
      key: track.key,
      name: track.name,
      description: track.description,
      duration: `~${Math.round(totalHours / 10)} wks`,
      modules: track.modules.length,
      lessons,
      color: track.color,
      icon: trackIcons[track.key] ?? Globe,
      modulesList: track.modules.map((m) => ({
        key: m.key,
        name: m.name,
        weeks: Math.max(1, Math.round(m.estimatedHours / 10)),
        lessons: m.lessons.length,
      })),
    }
  })
  
  const totalLessons = categoryTracks.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.modules.reduce((s, m) => s + m.lessons.length, 0), 0)
  const Icon = categoryIcons[category] ?? Globe

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1">
        {/* Category Hero */}
        <section className="py-16 sm:py-24 bg-muted/30">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <Link
                  href="/categories"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors mb-4"
                >
                  <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 rotate-180" aria-hidden="true" />
                  All Categories
                </Link>
                <div className={cn('mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl', getAccent(catInfo.color).icon)}>
                  <Icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
                </div>
                <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-5xl">{catInfo.name}</h1>
                <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-lg text-muted-foreground">{catInfo.description}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                <p>{tracks.length} learning paths</p>
                <p>{totalLessons} total lessons</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Tracks in this category */}
        <section className="py-16 sm:py-20">
          <Reveal className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:gap-8 lg:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
              {tracks.map((track) => (
                <Link key={track.key} href={`/learn/${track.key}`} className="group">
                  <Card className="relative overflow-hidden transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-xl h-full border-border/50">
                    <div className={cn('absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 h-1 bg-gradient-to-r', getAccent(track.color).gradient)} />
                    <CardHeader>
                      <div className={cn('flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium', getAccent(track.color).text)}>
                        <track.icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {track.name}
                      </div>
                      <CardTitle className="text-2xl">{track.name}</CardTitle>
                      <CardDescription className="text-base">{track.description}</CardDescription>
                    </CardHeader>
                    <CardContent className="space-y-4">
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-center">
                        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/50">
                          <p className="text-2xl font-bold">{track.duration}</p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Estimated Time</p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/50">
                          <p className="text-2xl font-bold">{track.modules}</p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Modules</p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/50">
                          <p className="text-2xl font-bold">{track.lessons}</p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Lessons</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <Separator />
                      <ul className="space-y-3" role="list">
                        {track.modulesList.map((module) => (
                          <li key={module.key} className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-muted/30 group-hover:bg-muted/50 transition-colors">
                            <div className={cn('flex-shrink-0 w-10 h-10 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center', getAccent(track.color).icon)}>
                              <Code2Icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                              <p className="font-medium text-sm">{module.name}</p>
                              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{module.weeks} weeks • {module.lessons} lessons</p>
                            </div>
                            <ArrowRight className="h-5 w-5 text-muted-foreground group-hover:text-foreground transition-colors flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                          </li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    </CardContent>
                    <CardFooter className="border-t-0 pt-0">
                      <Button className="w-full" variant="default">
                        Start {track.name} <ArrowRight className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Button>
                    </CardFooter>
                  </Card>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Reveal>
        </section>

        {/* CTA */}
        <section className="py-16 sm:py-20 bg-muted/30">
          <Reveal className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <Card className="relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500/10 via-violet-500/10 to-amber-500/10 border-border/50">
              <CardContent className="relative py-12 sm:py-16 text-center">
                <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
                  <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
                    Not sure where to start?
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-muted-foreground">
                    Every path is free, written, and self-paced. Start where you are.
                  </p>
                  <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 sm:flex-row">
                    <Button size="lg" asChild className="gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
                      <Link href="/learn">
                        Browse All Categories
                        <ArrowRight className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Link>
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="lg" variant="outline" asChild className="gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
                      <Link href="/roadmap">View Full Roadmap</Link>
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </Reveal>
        </section>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}
