===== FILE: src/app/categories/page.tsx =====
import { Metadata } from 'next'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { ArrowRight, Globe, Code2, Cpu, Smartphone, Brain, Cloud, Gamepad2, Monitor } from 'lucide-react'
import { trackCategories } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { tracks as curriculumTracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { cn, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'

const categoryIcons: Record<string, typeof Globe> = {
  'web-development': Globe,
  'programming-languages': Code2,
  'embedded-systems': Cpu,
  'mobile-development': Smartphone,
  'data-science': Brain,
  'devops-cloud': Cloud,
  'game-development': Gamepad2,
  'desktop-apps': Monitor,
}

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Learning Categories | Nyxora Sync',
  description: 'Explore all learning categories: Web Development, Programming Languages, Mobile Development, Data Science, DevOps, Game Development, and Desktop Apps.',
  openGraph: {
    title: 'Learning Categories | Nyxora Sync',
    description: 'Explore all learning categories: Web Development, Programming Languages, Mobile Development, Data Science, DevOps, Game Development, and Desktop Apps.',
  },
}

export default function CategoriesPage() {
  const categoriesWithTracks = trackCategories
    .filter((cat) => cat.id !== 'embedded-systems' && curriculumTracks.some((t) => t.category === cat.id))
    .map((category) => {
      const categoryTracks = curriculumTracks.filter((t) => t.category === category.id)
      const totalLessons = categoryTracks.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.modules.reduce((s, m) => s + m.lessons.length, 0), 0)
      const totalTracks = categoryTracks.length
      return { category, totalLessons, totalTracks }
    })

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1">
        {/* Hero */}
        <section className="py-16 sm:py-24 bg-muted/30">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 text-center">
            <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-5xl">
              Learning{' '}
              <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 via-violet-500 to-amber-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                Categories
              </span>
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto text-lg text-muted-foreground">
              Browse all learning categories. Each category contains multiple learning paths from beginner to mastery.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Categories Grid */}
        <section className="py-16 sm:py-20">
          <Reveal className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 2xl:grid-cols-5">
              {categoriesWithTracks.map(({ category, totalLessons, totalTracks }) => {
                const Icon = categoryIcons[category.id] ?? Globe
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={category.id}
                    href={`/categories/${category.id}`}
                    className="group relative block"
                  >
                    <Card className="relative h-full overflow-hidden transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-xl border-border/50">
                      <div
                        className={cn(
                          'absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-1 bg-gradient-to-r',
                          getAccent(category.color).gradient
                        )}
                      />
                      <CardContent className="pt-6 pb-8 flex flex-col h-full">
                        <div className={cn('mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl', getAccent(category.color).icon)}>
                          <Icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                          {category.name}
                        </h3>
                        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-1">
                          {category.description}
                        </p>
                        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground border-t border-border/50 pt-4">
                          <span>{totalTracks} tracks</span>
                          <span>{totalLessons} lessons</span>
                        </div>
                        <ArrowRight
                          className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 h-5 w-5 text-muted-foreground/50 group-hover:text-foreground group-hover:right-3 transition-all"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                      </CardContent>
                    </Card>
                  </Link>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </Reveal>
        </section>

        {/* CTA */}
        <section className="py-16 sm:py-20 bg-muted/30">
          <Reveal className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <Card className="relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500/10 via-violet-500/10 to-amber-500/10 border-border/50">
              <CardContent className="relative py-12 sm:py-16 text-center">
                <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
                  <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
                    Ready to start learning?
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-muted-foreground">
                    Pick a category above or browse all tracks on the Learn page.
                  </p>
                  <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 sm:flex-row">
                    <Button size="lg" asChild className="gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
                      <Link href="/learn">
                        Browse All Tracks
                        <ArrowRight className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Link>
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="lg" variant="outline" asChild className="gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
                      <Link href="/roadmap">View Full Roadmap</Link>
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </Reveal>
        </section>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/certificates/[verificationId]/page.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import { ArrowLeft, Award, BadgeCheck, CalendarDays, ExternalLink, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { CertificateNotice } from '@/components/certificates/certificate-notice'
import { PrintButton } from '@/components/certificates/print-button'
import { getCertificateByVerificationId } from '@/lib/certificates'
import { CERTIFICATE_DISCLAIMER, CERTIFICATE_DISCLAIMER_SHORT } from '@/lib/certificate-copy'
import { siteUrl } from '@/lib/seo'
import { shortDate } from '@/lib/time'

type PageProps = {
  params: Promise<{ verificationId: string }>
  searchParams: Promise<{ print?: string }>
}

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { verificationId } = await params
  const certificate = await getCertificateByVerificationId(verificationId)

  if (!certificate) {
    return { title: 'Certificate not found', robots: { index: false, follow: false } }
  }

  return {
    title: `${certificate.trackName} certificate`,
    description: `Informal, non-accredited Nyxora Sync completion record for ${certificate.recipientName ?? 'a learner'}. Showcase only; not a recognized qualification.`,
    robots: { index: false, follow: false },
  }
}

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export default async function CertificatePage({ params, searchParams }: PageProps) {
  const { verificationId } = await params
  const { print } = await searchParams
  const certificate = await getCertificateByVerificationId(verificationId)
  if (!certificate) notFound()

  const verifyUrl = `${siteUrl}/certificates/${certificate.verificationId}`
  const recipient = certificate.recipientName?.trim() || certificate.user.name?.trim() || 'Nyxora Sync Learner'

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 py-8 sm:py-12">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-6 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3" data-print-hide>
            <Button asChild variant="ghost" size="sm" className="gap-2">
              <Link href="/certificates">
                <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                My certificates
              </Link>
            </Button>
            <PrintButton auto={print === '1'} />
          </div>

          <article className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-2 border-emerald-600/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 via-background to-amber-50 p-6 shadow-xl sm:p-10 lg:p-14 dark:from-emerald-950/30 dark:via-background dark:to-amber-950/20">
            <div
              className="pointer-events-none absolute -right-20 -top-20 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/10 blur-3xl"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <div
              className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-24 -left-24 h-72 w-72 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 blur-3xl"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />

            <div className="relative space-y-8 text-center">
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-emerald-600/30 bg-emerald-500/10">
                  <Award className="h-9 w-9 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" aria-hidden="true" />
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">
                  Nyxora Sync
                </p>
                <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">Certificate of Completion</h1>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
                  Informal completion record · Not an accredited certificate
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <p className="text-sm uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">This is to acknowledge that</p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-5xl">{recipient}</p>
                <p className="mx-auto max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">
                  has successfully completed every lesson in the
                </p>
                <p className="text-2xl font-semibold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 sm:text-3xl">
                  {certificate.trackName}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-2xl grid-cols-1 gap-4 border-t border-border/60 pt-6 text-sm sm:grid-cols-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <CalendarDays className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <span>{shortDate(certificate.issuedAt.toISOString())}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <BadgeCheck className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-600" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <span>All lessons complete</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <span>ID {certificate.verificationId.slice(0, 12)}…</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/70 bg-background/60 p-4">
                <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
                  Verification link
                </p>
                <Link
                  href={`/certificates/${certificate.verificationId}`}
                  className="mt-1 inline-flex max-w-full items-center gap-1.5 break-all text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
                >
                  {verifyUrl}
                  <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                </Link>
                <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  This link confirms that the record exists on Nyxora Sync. It does not verify
                  identity, accreditation, or any formal qualification.
                </p>
              </div>

              <CertificateNotice className="mx-auto max-w-2xl text-left" text={CERTIFICATE_DISCLAIMER} />
            </div>
          </article>

          <p className="text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground" data-print-hide>
            {CERTIFICATE_DISCLAIMER_SHORT}
          </p>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/app/certificates/page.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { Award, CheckCircle2, ExternalLink, Lock, Printer } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Progress } from '@/components/ui/progress'
import { CertificateNotice } from '@/components/certificates/certificate-notice'
import { auth } from '@/lib/auth/auth'
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { syncUserCertificates, trackProgress } from '@/lib/certificates'
import { CERTIFICATE_ISSUANCE_NOTE } from '@/lib/certificate-copy'
import { shortDate } from '@/lib/time'

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Certificates',
  description:
    'Your Nyxora Sync track progress and informal, non-accredited completion certificates. For showcase only; not a recognized qualification.',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
}

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export default async function CertificatesPage() {
  const session = await auth()
  if (!session?.user?.id) redirect('/auth/signin?callbackUrl=/certificates')

  const [certificates, completedRows] = await Promise.all([
    syncUserCertificates(session.user.id),
    prisma.userProgress.findMany({
      where: { userId: session.user.id, completed: true },
      select: { lessonKey: true },
    }),
  ])

  const completedIds = new Set(completedRows.map((row) => row.lessonKey))
  const certificateByTrack = new Map(certificates.map((certificate) => [certificate.trackKey, certificate]))
  const inProgress = tracks
    .map((track) => ({
      track,
      progress: trackProgress(track.key, completedIds),
      certificate: certificateByTrack.get(track.key),
    }))
    .filter((entry) => !entry.certificate && entry.progress.completed > 0)
  const notStarted = tracks.filter((track) => {
    const progress = trackProgress(track.key, completedIds)
    return !certificateByTrack.has(track.key) && progress.completed === 0
  })

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1 py-10 sm:py-14">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <Badge variant="outline" className="gap-1.5">
              <Award className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              Showcase record
            </Badge>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">Certificates</h1>
            <p className="max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">
              Complete every lesson in a track to earn a printable certificate with a public
              verification link. {CERTIFICATE_ISSUANCE_NOTE}
            </p>
            <p className="max-w-2xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              These are informal completion records, not accredited certificates. Read the{' '}
              <Link href="/faq#certificates" className="font-medium text-foreground underline-offset-4 hover:underline">
                certificate FAQ
              </Link>{' '}
              before sharing one.
            </p>
          </div>

          <CertificateNotice className="mt-6 max-w-3xl" />

          {certificates.length > 0 && (
            <section className="mt-10 space-y-4" aria-labelledby="earned-certificates">
              <h2 id="earned-certificates" className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                Earned certificates ({certificates.length})
              </h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                {certificates.map((certificate) => (
                  <Card key={certificate.id} className="border-emerald-500/30">
                    <CardHeader>
                      <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {certificate.trackName}
                      </CardTitle>
                      <CardDescription>
                        Issued {shortDate(certificate.issuedAt)} · ID {certificate.verificationId.slice(0, 10)}…
                      </CardDescription>
                    </CardHeader>
                    <CardContent className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <Button asChild size="sm" className="gap-1.5">
                        <Link href={`/certificates/${certificate.verificationId}`}>
                          View certificate
                          <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        </Link>
                      </Button>
                      <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1.5">
                        <Link href={`/certificates/${certificate.verificationId}?print=1`}>
                          <Printer className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          Print
                        </Link>
                      </Button>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          {inProgress.length > 0 && (
            <section className="mt-12 space-y-4" aria-labelledby="in-progress-certificates">
              <h2 id="in-progress-certificates" className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                In progress
              </h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                {inProgress.map(({ track, progress }) => (
                  <Card key={track.key}>
                    <CardHeader className="pb-3">
                      <CardTitle className="text-base">{track.name}</CardTitle>
                      <CardDescription>
                        {progress.completed} of {progress.total} lessons complete
                      </CardDescription>
                    </CardHeader>
                    <CardContent className="space-y-3">
                      <Progress value={progress.percent} aria-label={`${track.name} ${progress.percent}% complete`} />
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="text-sm font-medium">{progress.percent}%</span>
                        <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline">
                          <Link href={`/learn/${track.key}`}>Continue track</Link>
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          {certificates.length === 0 && inProgress.length === 0 && (
            <Card className="mt-10">
              <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-14 text-center">
                <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
                  <Lock className="h-7 w-7 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-lg font-semibold">Your first certificate is one track away</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 max-w-md text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    Complete a lesson to start tracking progress. A certificate unlocks when every
                    lesson in a track is complete and your progress has synced to your account.
                    It is an informal, non-accredited record for showcase only.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Button asChild>
                  <Link href="/learn">Start learning</Link>
                </Button>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}

          {notStarted.length > 0 && (certificates.length > 0 || inProgress.length > 0) && (
            <section className="mt-12 space-y-4" aria-labelledby="available-certificates">
              <h2 id="available-certificates" className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                Available tracks
              </h2>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {notStarted.map((track) => (
                  <Button key={track.key} asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
                    <Link href={`/learn/${track.key}`}>{track.name}</Link>
                  </Button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/app/code-of-conduct/page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import { LegalPage } from '@/components/legal-page'
import { NEWSLETTER_ENABLED } from '@/lib/newsletter-feature'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

export const metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Code of Conduct',
  description:
    'The rules for participating in Nyxora Sync: lesson discussions, the project showcase, profiles, GitHub, and every community space.',
  path: '/code-of-conduct',
})

export default function CodeOfConductPage() {
  return (
    <LegalPage
      badge="Community"
      title="Code of Conduct"
      intro="The short version: be decent, be specific, and do not make the room smaller for the person after you."
      updated="October 20, 2026"
    >
      <p>
        Nyxora Sync is a learning space. People arrive with different backgrounds, languages, time
        zones, and confidence levels. The rules below exist so that asking a basic question never
        costs anyone their dignity.
      </p>

      <h2>Our pledge</h2>
      <p>
        We pledge to make participation welcoming for everyone, regardless of experience level,
        age, body size, visible or invisible disability, ethnicity, sex characteristics, gender
        identity and expression, education, socio-economic status, nationality, personal
        appearance, race, religion, or sexual identity and orientation.
      </p>

      <h2>Expected behavior</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>Be respectful of differing viewpoints and experiences.</li>
        <li>Give specific, actionable feedback: point at the sentence or line, not the person.</li>
        <li>Assume good faith, then ask a clarifying question before escalating.</li>
        <li>Respect privacy. Do not share someone&apos;s code, error logs, or personal details without permission.</li>
        <li>Credit sources when you reuse an explanation, diagram, or resource.</li>
        <li>Accept correction gracefully. Being wrong about an API is normal; doubling down is a choice.</li>
      </ul>

      <h2>Unacceptable behavior</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>Harassment, intimidation, stalking, or following someone across threads after they asked you to stop.</li>
        <li>Discriminatory jokes, slurs, or imagery, including &ldquo;just a joke&rdquo; versions.</li>
        <li>Sexual attention or advances in any community space.</li>
        <li>Publishing private information (doxxing), including addresses, emails, or credentials.</li>
        <li>Deliberate misinformation, plagiarism, or presenting someone else&apos;s project as your own.</li>
        <li>Spam, undisclosed promotion, recruiting, or repeated off-topic links.</li>
        <li>Posting malware, phishing links, or code intended to harm a reader&apos;s machine.</li>
        <li>Retaliating against someone who reported a problem.</li>
      </ul>

      <h2>Scope</h2>
      <p>
        This code applies in lesson discussions, the project showcase, public profiles, GitHub
        issues and pull requests, contact messages{NEWSLETTER_ENABLED && ', the newsletter'}, and
        any space where you are representing Nyxora Sync.
      </p>

      <h2>Reporting</h2>
      <p>
        Report conduct problems through the{' '}
        <Link href="/contact">contact form</Link> and choose the &ldquo;Code of conduct report&rdquo;
        topic. Include what happened, where, when, and links or screenshots if you have them. If the
        report involves a maintainer, say so; they will be excluded from handling it.
      </p>
      <p>
        Reports are kept confidential within the people handling them. We will acknowledge a report
        as soon as we can and avoid acting on conflicts of interest. We may not be able to act on an
        anonymous report that lacks enough detail to identify the behavior.
      </p>
      <p>
        For security vulnerabilities, do not open a public issue. Use the repository&apos;s security
        reporting flow and see the <Link href="/contact">contact page</Link> for the current route.
      </p>

      <h2>Enforcement</h2>
      <p>Enforcement is proportional to impact and history. A typical progression:</p>
      <ol>
        <li>
          <strong>Correction.</strong> A private note explaining what was inappropriate and why,
          with a request to change the behavior.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Warning.</strong> A recorded warning with consequences for repeating the behavior.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Temporary ban.</strong> A time-limited removal from community spaces, with the
          length matched to severity.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Permanent ban.</strong> Removal for severe or repeated violations, harassment, or
          retaliation.
        </li>
      </ol>
      <p>
        Severe behavior can skip directly to a temporary or permanent ban. Decisions are documented
        so they stay consistent, and the person affected is told what happened unless doing so would
        expose the reporter to harm.
      </p>

      <h2>Attribution</h2>
      <p>
        This code is adapted from the{' '}
        <a href="https://www.contributor-covenant.org/version/2/1/code_of_conduct/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
          Contributor Covenant, version 2.1
        </a>
        , available under CC BY 4.0. The enforcement section is shortened for a small open-source
        project.
      </p>
    </LegalPage>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/community/page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { CommunityStats } from '@/components/community/community-stats'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import {
  Users, MessageCircle, Rocket, Heart, Star,
  ArrowRight, ExternalLink, Code2, HandHeart,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { GithubIcon } from '@/components/icons/brand'
import { cn, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

export const metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Community',
  description: 'Learn MERN alongside other developers: lesson discussions, project showcase, public profiles, and open-source contribution.',
  path: '/community',
  keywords: ['MERN community', 'developer showcase', 'learn to code with others'],
})

const channels = [
  {
    name: 'Project Showcase',
    description: 'Real apps built by learners. Ask how they work, leave feedback, or share your own.',
    icon: Rocket,

import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton'

export default function Loading() {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <Card className="overflow-hidden">
        <div className="h-24 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500/20 via-blue-500/10 to-violet-500/20" aria-hidden="true" />
        <CardContent className="relative pt-0">
          <div className="-mt-10 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-end gap-4">
              <Skeleton className="h-20 w-20 rounded-full border-4 border-background" />
              <div className="pb-1">
                <Skeleton className="h-8 w-48" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-32 mt-2" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
          <Skeleton key={i} className="h-32" />
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
          <Card key={i}>
            <CardContent className="pt-5">
              <Skeleton className="h-28 w-full" />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
          <Skeleton key={i} className="h-64" />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'

  title: 'Learner Profile',
  description: 'A Nyxora Sync community profile: shared projects, discussions, and learning progress.',
  robots: { index: false, follow: true },
}

// The profile id is only known at request time; render the shell on demand.

export default async function ProfilePage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>
}) {
  const { id } = await params

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />

          <ProfileClientWrapper id={id} />
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

import dynamic from 'next/dynamic'

const ProfileClient = dynamic(
  () => import('@/components/community/profile-client').then((mod) => mod.ProfileClient),
  {
    loading: () => (
      <div className="space-y-8">
        <div className="h-40 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500/20 via-blue-500/10 to-violet-500/20 animate-pulse rounded-xl" />
        <div className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/30" />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => <div key={i} className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/30" />)}
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
    ssr: false,
  }
)

export function ProfileClientWrapper({ id }: { id: string }) {
  return <ProfileClient id={id} />
}

            <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Badges */}

              <Skeleton className="h-10 w-10 rounded-lg mx-auto mb-2" />
              <Skeleton className="h-4 w-24 mx-auto mb-1" />
              <Skeleton className="h-3 w-20 mx-auto" />

          <Skeleton key={i} className="h-16 rounded-lg border" />

      </div>

  )

===== FILE: src/app/error.tsx =====
'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Home, RotateCcw, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Error({
  error,
  reset,
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string }
  reset: () => void
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    // Log the error to an error reporting service
    console.error('Application error:', error)
  }, [error])

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="relative flex-1 overflow-hidden">
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 -z-10 bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-500/5 via-background to-rose-500/10"
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-0 -z-10 h-72 w-72 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-rose-500/10 blur-3xl"
          aria-hidden="true"
        />

        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-full max-w-4xl flex-col justify-center px-4 py-16 sm:px-6 sm:py-20 lg:px-8">
          <div className="text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/20 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-600 shadow-sm dark:text-rose-400">
              <AlertCircle className="h-10 w-10" aria-hidden="true" />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-5 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
              500 · Server Error
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
              Something went wrong.
            </h1>
            <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-lg text-muted-foreground">
              We encountered an unexpected error and logged the details. Please try again in a few
              moments or go back to the homepage.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
              <Button onClick={() => reset()} className="gap-2">
                <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                Try again
              </Button>
              <Button variant="outline" asChild>
                <Link href="/">
                  <Home className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Go home
                </Link>
              </Button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border/60 bg-muted/20 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <svg
                xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                width="24"
                height="24"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                fill="none"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="2"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeLinejoin="round"
                className="h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0"
                aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <path d="M12 15v5s3.03-.55 4-2c1.08-1.62 0-5 0-5" />
                <path d="M4.5 16.5c-1.5 1.26-2 5-2 5s3.74-.5 5-2c.71-.84.7-2.13-.09-2.91a2.18 2.18 0 0 0-2.91-.09" />
              </svg>
              Looking for a learner project?{' '}
              <Link
                href="/community/showcase"
                className="font-medium text-foreground underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
              >
                Browse the showcase
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/app/faq/page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import { ChevronDown, HelpCircle, MessageCircle, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { FaqHashOpener } from '@/components/faq/faq-hash-opener'
import { JsonLd } from '@/components/structured-data'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { FAQ_LAST_REVIEWED, faqSections } from '@/lib/faq'
import { absoluteUrl, pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

export const metadata: Metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Frequently Asked Questions',
  description:
    'Answers about Nyxora Sync: cost, accounts, tracks, progress, certificates, the playground, contributing, accessibility, and licenses.',
  path: '/faq',
})

const reviewedDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  dateStyle: 'long',
  timeZone: 'UTC',
}).format(new Date(`${FAQ_LAST_REVIEWED}T00:00:00Z`))

const faqSchema = {
  '@context': 'https://schema.org',
  '@type': 'FAQPage',
  url: absoluteUrl('/faq'),
  dateModified: `${FAQ_LAST_REVIEWED}T00:00:00.000Z`,
  mainEntity: faqSections.flatMap((section) =>
    section.items.map((item) => ({
      '@type': 'Question',
      name: item.question,
      acceptedAnswer: {
        '@type': 'Answer',
        text: item.answer,
      },
    }))
  ),
}

export default function FaqPage() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <JsonLd data={faqSchema} />
      <FaqHashOpener />
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1">
        <section className="border-b border-border/50 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-500/5 via-background to-emerald-500/5 py-12 sm:py-16">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <Badge variant="outline" className="mb-4">
              <HelpCircle className="mr-1.5 h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              Help
            </Badge>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
              Frequently asked questions
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-muted-foreground">
              Short answers about the lessons, accounts, progress, certificates, community, and the
              limits of a free curriculum. If your question is not here, the contact form is open.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Last reviewed <time dateTime={FAQ_LAST_REVIEWED}>{reviewedDate}</time>
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 sm:py-14 lg:px-8">
          <div className="space-y-12">
            {faqSections.map((section) => (
              <section key={section.id} id={section.id} aria-labelledby={`${section.id}-heading`}>
                <div className="mb-5">
                  <h2 id={`${section.id}-heading`} className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                    {section.title}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{section.description}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {section.items.map((item) => (
                    <details
                      key={item.slug}
                      id={item.slug}
                      className="faq-item group scroll-mt-24 rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-card transition-colors open:border-border open:bg-card"
                    >
                      <summary className="flex cursor-pointer list-none items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-xl px-5 py-4 font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-muted/50 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 [&::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden">
                        <span>{item.question}</span>
                        <ChevronDown
                          className="faq-chevron h-5 w-5 flex-shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                      </summary>
                      <div className="faq-answer border-t border-border/50 px-5 pb-5 pt-4">
                        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">{item.answer}</p>
                        {item.related && (
                          <Link
                            href={item.related.href}
                            className="mt-3 inline-flex text-sm font-medium text-emerald-700 underline-offset-4 hover:underline dark:text-emerald-400"
                          >
                            {item.related.label}
                          </Link>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </details>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </section>
            ))}
          </div>

          <aside className="mt-14 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/5 p-6 sm:p-8">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                <MessageCircle className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Still stuck?</h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  Ask in the lesson discussion, open an issue for a content or code problem, or send
                  a private message about privacy and account requests.
                </p>
                <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                  <Button asChild size="sm">
                    <Link href="/contact">Contact the maintainers</Link>
                  </Button>
                  <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline">
                    <Link href="/community">Browse the community</Link>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </aside>

          <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            Search can find individual answers across the whole site.
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

const organizationId = `${siteUrl}/#organization`

import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'

export default function Loading() {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Skeleton className="h-6 w-48" />
        <Skeleton className="h-8 w-64" />
        <Skeleton className="h-3 w-48" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <Skeleton className="h-6 w-32 mb-4" />
            <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-24 mt-4" />
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <Skeleton className="h-6 w-32 mb-4" />
            <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-24 mt-4" />
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="pt-6">
            <Skeleton className="h-6 w-32 mb-4" />
            <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-24 mt-4" />
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4">

              <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />

          </Card>

      </div>

===== FILE: src/app/learn/[track]/[module]/[lesson]/page.tsx =====
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import type { Metadata } from 'next'

import type { ComponentType } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { getTrack, getModule, getLesson, tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { JsonLd } from '@/components/structured-data'
import { absoluteUrl, canonicalLearnPath, canonicalTrackForModule, pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'
import { LessonClient } from '@/components/lesson-client'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { formatLessonUpdated, getLessonUpdated } from '@/lib/lesson-updates'
import { Clock, Zap, BookOpen, Construction, CalendarDays } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn, getDifficultyColor, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'

export function generateStaticParams() {
  const params: { track: string; module: string; lesson: string }[] = []
  for (const track of tracks) {
    for (const m of track.modules) {
      for (const lesson of m.lessons) {
        params.push({ track: track.key, module: m.key, lesson: lesson.id })
      }
    }
  }
  return params
}

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ track: string; module: string; lesson: string }>
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { track: trackKey, module: moduleKey, lesson: lessonId } = await params
  const track = getTrack(trackKey)
  const mod = getModule(trackKey, moduleKey)
  const lesson = getLesson(trackKey, moduleKey, lessonId)

  if (!track || !mod || !lesson) {
    return { title: 'Lesson not found', robots: { index: false, follow: false } }
  }

  const canonicalTrack = getTrack(canonicalTrackForModule(track.key, mod.key)) ?? track

  return pageMetadata({
    title: lesson.title,
    description: `${lesson.description} A free ${lesson.difficulty} lesson in the ${mod.name} module of the ${canonicalTrack.name} on Nyxora Sync.`,
    path: canonicalLearnPath(track.key, mod.key, lesson.id),
    type: 'article',
    keywords: [lesson.title, mod.name, track.name, 'MERN lesson', 'free coding lesson'],
  })
}

export default async function LessonPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ track: string; module: string; lesson: string }>
}) {
  const { track: trackKey, module: moduleKey, lesson: lessonId } = await params
  const track = getTrack(trackKey)
  const mod = getModule(trackKey, moduleKey)
  const lesson = getLesson(trackKey, moduleKey, lessonId)

  if (!track || !mod || !lesson) notFound()

  // Compute prev/next lessons across the whole track
  const allLessons = track.modules.flatMap((m) =>
    m.lessons.map((l) => ({ ...l, moduleKey: m.key, moduleName: m.name }))
  )
  const currentIndex = allLessons.findIndex((l) => l.id === lesson.id)
  const prev = currentIndex > 0 ? allLessons[currentIndex - 1] : null
  const next = currentIndex < allLessons.length - 1 ? allLessons[currentIndex + 1] : null

  const difficultyColor = getDifficultyColor(lesson.difficulty)

  // Written lesson content is authored as MDX. Not every lesson has been
  // written yet, so missing files fall back to a friendly notice.
  let LessonContent: ComponentType | null = null
  try {
    const mdx = await import(`@/content/lessons/${lesson.id}.mdx`)
    LessonContent = mdx.default as ComponentType
  } catch {
    LessonContent = null
  }

  const lessonPath = `/learn/${track.key}/${mod.key}/${lesson.id}`
  const canonicalPath = canonicalLearnPath(track.key, mod.key, lesson.id)
  const canonicalTrackKey = canonicalTrackForModule(track.key, mod.key)
  const canonicalTrack = getTrack(canonicalTrackKey) ?? track
  const updated = getLessonUpdated(lesson.id)
  const breadcrumbSchema = {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'BreadcrumbList',
    itemListElement: [
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 1, name: 'Home', item: absoluteUrl('/') },
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 2, name: 'Learn', item: absoluteUrl('/learn') },
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 3, name: track.name, item: absoluteUrl(`/learn/${track.key}`) },
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 4, name: mod.name, item: absoluteUrl(`/learn/${track.key}/${mod.key}`) },
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 5, name: lesson.title, item: absoluteUrl(lessonPath) },
    ],
  }
  const learningResourceSchema = {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'LearningResource',
    name: lesson.title,
    description: lesson.description,
    url: absoluteUrl(canonicalPath),
    learningResourceType: 'Lesson',
    educationalLevel: lesson.difficulty,
    isAccessibleForFree: true,
    inLanguage: 'en',
    timeRequired: `PT${lesson.estimatedMinutes}M`,
    ...(updated ? { dateModified: `${updated}T00:00:00.000Z` } : {}),
    teaches: lesson.keyTakeaways,
    isPartOf: {
      '@type': 'Course',
      name: canonicalTrack.name,
      url: absoluteUrl(`/learn/${canonicalTrackKey}`),
    },
    provider: {
      '@type': 'Organization',
      name: 'Nyxora Sync',
      url: absoluteUrl('/'),
    },
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <JsonLd data={[breadcrumbSchema, learningResourceSchema]} />
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 sm:py-14">
          {/* Breadcrumb */}
          <nav className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-6" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
            <ol className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
              <li>
                <Link href="/learn" className="hover:text-foreground transition-colors">Learn</Link>
              </li>
              <li aria-hidden="true">/</li>
              <li>
                <Link href={`/learn/${track.key}`} className="hover:text-foreground transition-colors">{track.name}</Link>
              </li>
              <li aria-hidden="true">/</li>
              <li>
                <Link href={`/learn/${track.key}/${mod.key}`} className="hover:text-foreground transition-colors">{mod.name}</Link>
              </li>
              <li aria-hidden="true">/</li>
              <li className="text-foreground font-medium truncate max-w-[200px]" aria-current="page">{lesson.title}</li>
            </ol>
          </nav>

          {/* Lesson header */}
          <header className="mb-10">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 flex-wrap">
              <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">
                Lesson {lesson.order} of {mod.lessons.length}
              </Badge>
              <Badge
                variant="outline"
                className={cn('text-xs', getAccent(difficultyColor).borderSoft, getAccent(difficultyColor).text)}
              >
                {lesson.difficulty}
              </Badge>
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">{lesson.title}</h1>
            <p className="mt-3 text-lg text-muted-foreground">{lesson.description}</p>
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Clock className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                {lesson.estimatedMinutes} min estimated
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Zap className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                {lesson.xpReward} XP
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                {lesson.resources.length} curated resources
              </span>
              {updated && (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <CalendarDays className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Last updated{' '}
                  <time dateTime={updated}>{formatLessonUpdated(updated)}</time>
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </header>

          <LessonClient
            lesson={lesson}
            track={track.key}
            moduleKey={mod.key}
            prev={prev}
            next={next}
          >
            {LessonContent ? (
              <LessonContent />
            ) : (
              <Card className="border-dashed bg-muted/20">
                <CardContent className="flex gap-3 pt-6">
                  <Construction className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-semibold">Full written lesson coming soon</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      We are writing this lesson directly on the site. Until then, the curated
                      resources below cover the topic end to end.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )}
          </LessonClient>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/learn/[track]/page.tsx =====
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import type { Metadata } from 'next'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { getTrack, tracks, getTotalXP, getTotalMinutes } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { JsonLd } from '@/components/structured-data'
import { absoluteUrl, pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { TrackProgressClient } from '@/components/track-progress-client'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import { BookOpen, Code2, Server, Database, Atom, Layers, FileCode, FlaskConical, ShieldCheck, Container, Network, Gauge, Rocket, Cloud, Boxes, Smartphone, Sparkles, ArrowRight, Clock, Zap, Braces, Hash, Terminal, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'

const iconMap: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  BookOpen, Code2, Server, Database, Atom, Layers,
  FileCode, FlaskConical, ShieldCheck, Container, Network, Gauge, Rocket,
  Cloud, Boxes, Smartphone, Sparkles, Braces, Hash, Terminal,
}

export function generateStaticParams() {
  return tracks.map((track) => ({ track: track.key }))
}

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ track: string }>
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { track: trackKey } = await params
  const track = getTrack(trackKey)

  if (!track) {
    return { title: 'Track not found', robots: { index: false, follow: false } }
  }

  return pageMetadata({
    title: track.name,
    description: track.description,
    path: `/learn/${track.key}`,
    keywords:
      track.category === 'programming-languages'
        ? [track.name, 'learn ' + track.name, 'programming', 'beginner to mastery', 'free coding course']
        : [track.name, 'MERN curriculum', 'learn MERN', 'MongoDB', 'Express', 'React', 'Node.js'],
  })
}

export default async function TrackPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ track: string }> }) {
  const { track: trackKey } = await params
  const track = getTrack(trackKey)

  if (!track) notFound()

  const totalXP = getTotalXP(track.key)
  const totalMinutes = getTotalMinutes(track.key)
  const totalHours = Math.round(totalMinutes / 60)
  const totalLessons = track.modules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.lessons.length, 0)
  const educationalLevel =
    track.key === 'beginner'
      ? 'Beginner'
      : track.key === 'specializations'
        ? 'Advanced'
        : track.category === 'programming-languages'
          ? 'Beginner to mastery'
          : 'Beginner to advanced'
  const TrackIcon = iconMap[track.icon] || BookOpen

  const trackPath = `/learn/${track.key}`
  const courseSchema = {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'Course',
    name: `${track.name} — Nyxora Sync`,
    description: track.description,
    url: absoluteUrl(trackPath),
    provider: {
      '@type': 'Organization',
      name: 'Nyxora Sync',

      url: absoluteUrl('/'),
    },
    isAccessibleForFree: true,
    inLanguage: 'en',
    educationalLevel,
    teaches: track.modules.map((module) => module.name),
    offers: {
      '@type': 'Offer',
      category: 'Free',
      price: 0,
      priceCurrency: 'USD',
      availability: 'https://schema.org/InStock',
    },
    hasCourseInstance: {
      '@type': 'CourseInstance',
      courseMode: 'online',
      courseWorkload: `PT${totalHours}H`,
    },
  }
  const breadcrumbSchema = {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'BreadcrumbList',
    itemListElement: [
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 1, name: 'Home', item: absoluteUrl('/') },
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 2, name: 'Learn', item: absoluteUrl('/learn') },
      { '@type': 'ListItem', position: 3, name: track.name, item: absoluteUrl(trackPath) },
    ],
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <JsonLd data={[breadcrumbSchema, courseSchema]} />
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1">
        {/* Track Header */}
        <section className="py-12 sm:py-16 border-b border-border/50">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <Link
              href="/learn"
              className="inline-flex items-center text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground mb-6 transition-colors"
            >
              ← All tracks
            </Link>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
              <div className="max-w-2xl">
                <div
                  className={cn('inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium mb-4', getAccent(track.color).pill)}
                >
                  <TrackIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {track.name}
                </div>
                <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">{track.name}</h1>
                <p className="mt-3 text-lg text-muted-foreground">{track.description}</p>
                <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Layers className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {track.modules.length} modules
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {totalLessons} lessons
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Clock className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    ~{totalHours} hours
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Zap className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {totalXP} XP available
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Progress card (client) */}
              <TrackProgressClient trackKey={track.key} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Modules List */}
        <section className="py-12 sm:py-16">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-8">Curriculum</h2>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {track.modules
                .filter((module) => module.status !== 'advanced' && module.status !== 'planned')
                .map((module, index) => {
                const Icon = iconMap[module.icon] || Layers
                const moduleLessons = module.lessons.length
                const moduleXP = module.lessons.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.xpReward, 0)
                return (
                  <Reveal key={module.id} delay={Math.min(index * 0.04, 0.24)}>
                    <Link
                      href={`/learn/${track.key}/${module.key}`}
                      className="group block"
                    >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-5 sm:p-6 transition-all motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-border hover:shadow-md">
                      <div
                        className={cn('hidden sm:flex h-14 w-14 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl', getAccent(track.color).icon)}
                      >
                        <Icon className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
                          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                            Module {index + 1}
                          </span>
                          <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">
                            {module.estimatedHours}h
                          </Badge>
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                          {module.name}
                        </h3>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground line-clamp-2">
                          {module.description}
                        </p>
                        <div className="mt-2 flex gap-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          <span>{moduleLessons} lessons</span>
                          <span>{moduleXP} XP</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <ArrowRight
                        className="h-5 w-5 flex-shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1 group-hover:text-foreground"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      />
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  </Reveal>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/learn/languages/page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import { ArrowRight, BookOpen, Code2, Layers, Rocket } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { LanguagePaths } from '@/components/language-paths'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

const languages = tracks.filter((track) => track.category === 'programming-languages')

const lessonCount = new Set(
  languages.flatMap((track) =>
    track.modules.flatMap((module) => module.lessons.map((lesson) => lesson.id))
  )
).size

import Link from 'next/link'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator'
import { BookOpen, Code2, ArrowRight, CheckCircle, Clock, Users, Trophy, Code2 as Code2Icon, Sparkles, Smartphone, Brain, Cloud, Gamepad2, Monitor, Globe } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { LanguagePaths } from '@/components/language-paths'
import { SpecializationsBanner } from '@/components/specializations-banner'
import { Reveal } from '@/components/motion/reveal'
import { cn, getAccent } from '@/lib/utils'
import { tracks as curriculumTracks, trackCategories } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

const categoryIcons: Record<string, typeof Globe> = {
  'web-development': Globe,
  'programming-languages': Code2,
  'mobile-development': Smartphone,
  'data-science': Brain,
  'devops-cloud': Cloud,
  'game-development': Gamepad2,
  'desktop-apps': Monitor,
}

const trackIcons: Record<string, typeof BookOpen> = {
  beginner: BookOpen,
  developer: Code2,
  specializations: Sparkles,
  c: BookOpen,
  cpp: Code2,
  csharp: Code2,
  python: BookOpen,
}

const tracks = curriculumTracks.map((track) => {
  const coreModules = track.modules.filter((m) => m.status !== 'advanced' && m.status !== 'planned')
  const lessons = coreModules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.lessons.length, 0)
  const totalHours = coreModules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.estimatedHours, 0)
  return {
    key: track.key,
    name: track.name,
    description: track.description,
    category: track.category,
    duration: `~${Math.round(totalHours / 10)} wks`,
    modules: coreModules.length,
    lessons,
    color: track.color,
    icon: trackIcons[track.key] ?? BookOpen,
    modulesList: coreModules.map((m) => ({
      key: m.key,
      name: m.name,
      weeks: Math.max(1, Math.round(m.estimatedHours / 10)),
      lessons: m.lessons.length,
    })),
  }
})

const uniqueLessonCount = new Set(
  curriculumTracks.flatMap((track) =>
    track.modules.flatMap((mod) => mod.lessons.map((lesson) => lesson.id))
  )
).size

export const metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Explore All Learning Paths',
  description: `Choose from ${trackCategories.filter(c => curriculumTracks.some(t => t.category === c.id)).length} categories with ${uniqueLessonCount} free written lessons across web development, programming languages, embedded systems, and more. Track progress and earn informal, non-accredited completion certificates.`,
  path: '/learn',
  keywords: ['web development', 'programming languages', 'embedded systems', 'IoT', 'Arduino', 'ESP32', 'C', 'C++', 'C#', 'Python', 'MERN', 'React', 'Node.js', 'free coding curriculum'],
})

===== FILE: src/app/newsletter/confirm/page.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { NewsletterActionPage } from '@/components/newsletter-action-page'
import { NEWSLETTER_ENABLED } from '@/lib/newsletter-feature'

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Confirm newsletter subscription',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
}

// The token only exists at request time; render this page on demand.

export default async function NewsletterConfirmPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ token?: string | string[] }>
}) {
  if (!NEWSLETTER_ENABLED) redirect('/')

  const { token } = await searchParams
  return (
    <NewsletterActionPage
      mode="confirm"
      token={typeof token === 'string' ? token : null}
    />
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/newsletter/unsubscribe/page.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { NewsletterActionPage } from '@/components/newsletter-action-page'
import { NEWSLETTER_ENABLED } from '@/lib/newsletter-feature'

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Unsubscribe from the newsletter',
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
}

// The token only exists at request time; render this page on demand.

export default async function NewsletterUnsubscribePage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ token?: string | string[] }>
}) {
  if (!NEWSLETTER_ENABLED) redirect('/')

  const { token } = await searchParams
  return (
    <NewsletterActionPage
      mode="unsubscribe"
      token={typeof token === 'string' ? token : null}
    />
  )
}

import { Metadata } from 'next'
import Link from 'next/link'

  noIndex: true,

interface PlannedTrack {
  key: string
  name: string
  description: string
  category: string
  color: string
  estimatedLessons: number
  estimatedHours: number
  status: 'planned' | 'in-progress' | 'needs-contributors'
  modules: { name: string; description: string }[]
  notes?: string
}

function StatusBadge({ status, color = 'blue' }: { status: string; color?: string }) {
  const variants: Record<string, string> = {
    planned: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-900/30 dark:text-amber-400',
    'in-progress': 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 dark:bg-blue-900/30 dark:text-blue-400',
    'needs-contributors': 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-900/30 dark:text-rose-400',
    advanced: 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-800 dark:bg-violet-900/30 dark:text-violet-400',
  }
  return (
    <span className={cn('inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium', variants[status] ?? variants.planned)}>
      {status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + status.slice(1).replace('-', ' ')}
    </span>
  )
}
