  X,
  Star,
  Zap,
  TrendingUp,
  Target,
  Award,
  RefreshCw,
  Play,
  Trophy,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useProgress } from '@/hooks/use-progress'
import {
  getDueFlashcards,
  getDailyKata,
  getAllFlashcardsByCategory,
  getAllKatas,
  getAllMicroProjects,
  getAllInterviewDrills,
  getFlashcard,
  getKata,
  getMicroProject,
  getInterviewDrill,
  type Flashcard,
  type CodeKata,
  type MicroProject,
  type InterviewDrill,
  type PracticeItem,
  type FlashcardProgress,
  sm2,
  initialFlashcardProgress,
} from '@/lib/practice'
import type { PracticeProgress } from '@/lib/practice'

const CATEGORY_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  javascript: 'bg-yellow-500',
  react: 'bg-blue-500',
  nodejs: 'bg-green-500',
  mongodb: 'bg-green-700',
  typescript: 'bg-blue-700',
  testing: 'bg-purple-500',
  security: 'bg-red-500',
  devops: 'bg-cyan-500',
  architecture: 'bg-indigo-500',
  'first-client': 'bg-violet-500',
}

const CATEGORY_ICONS: Record<string, any> = {
  javascript: BookOpen,
  react: Code2,
  nodejs: FolderOpen,
  mongodb: FolderOpen,
  typescript: Code2,
  testing: Mic,
  security: Zap,
  devops: Brain,
  architecture: Target,
  'first-client': Award,
}

function FlashcardReview() {
  const { practice, gradeFlashcard } = useProgress()
  const [flashcards, setFlashcards] = useState<Flashcard[]>([])
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0)
  const [showAnswer, setShowAnswer] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const due = getDueFlashcards(practice)
    setFlashcards(due)
    setLoading(false)
  }, [practice])

  const handleGrade = (grade: number) => {
    const card = flashcards[currentIndex]
    if (!card) return

    const progress = practice.flashcards[card.id] ?? initialFlashcardProgress
    const result = sm2(grade, progress)

    gradeFlashcard(card.id, grade, card.xpReward)

    setShowAnswer(false)

    if (currentIndex < flashcards.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex((i) => i + 1)
    } else {
      // Refresh the list after completing
      setFlashcards(getDueFlashcards(practice))
      setCurrentIndex(0)
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
        <Brain className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 animate-spin" />
        <p className="mt-2">Loading flashcards...</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (flashcards.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="text-center py-12">
        <CheckCircle className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-green-500" />
        <h3 className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold">No cards due today!</h3>
        <p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">
          All caught up. Check back tomorrow or practice katas below.
        </p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const card = flashcards[currentIndex]
  const progress = practice.flashcards[card.id] ?? initialFlashcardProgress

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        <span>Card {currentIndex + 1} of {flashcards.length}</span>
        <span className={CATEGORY_COLORS[card.category] + ' px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium'}>
          {card.category}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        className="relative h-64 cursor-pointer rounded-xl border-2 border-border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm transition-all hover:shadow-md"
        onClick={() => setShowAnswer(true)}
        role="button"
        tabIndex={0}
        onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && setShowAnswer(true)}
      >
        <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-center">
            <p className="text-foreground">{showAnswer ? card.answer : card.question}</p>
            {!showAnswer && (
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Click or press Enter to reveal</p>
            )}
            {showAnswer && (
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">Answer shown</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {showAnswer && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-end justify-center p-4 pointer-events-none">
            <div className="flex gap-2 pointer-events-auto">
              <button
                onClick={() => handleGrade(0)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-500 text-white text-sm hover:bg-red-600"
                title="Again (0)"
              >
                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleGrade(1)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-orange-500 text-white text-sm hover:bg-orange-600"
                title="Hard (1)"
              >
                <Clock className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleGrade(2)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-green-500 text-white text-sm hover:bg-green-600"
                title="Good (2)"
              >
                <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleGrade(3)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-500 text-white text-sm hover:bg-blue-600"
                title="Easy (3)"
              >
                <Star className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <span>Difficulty: {card.difficulty}</span>
        <span>~{card.estimatedMinutes} min</span>
        <span>XP: +{card.xpReward}</span>
        {progress.repetitions > 0 && (
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-100 dark:bg-purple-900 text-purple-800 dark:text-purple-200">
            Rep: {progress.repetitions} · Next: {new Date(progress.nextReview).toLocaleDateString()}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function KataOfTheDay() {
  const { practice, completeKata } = useProgress()
  const [kata, setKata] = useState<CodeKata | undefined>(undefined)
  const [code, setCode] = useState('')
  const [output, setOutput] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const dailyKata = getDailyKata()
    setKata(dailyKata)
    if (dailyKata?.starterCode) {
      setCode(dailyKata.starterCode)
    }
  }, [practice])

  const kataProgress = kata ? practice.katas[kata.id] : null

  if (!kata) {
    return (
      <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
        <Code2 className="mx-auto h-8 w-8" />
        <p className="mt-2">No katas available</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const runCode = () => {
    setRunning(true)
    setOutput('Running...')
    // Simulate execution - in real app would use a sandbox
    setTimeout(() => {
      const passed = kata.testCases.map((tc) => {
        // Simple check - in reality would execute code
        return `${tc.description}: ✓`
      }).join('\n')
      setOutput(`Tests passed:\n${passed}\n\nNote: Full execution requires sandbox environment.`)
      setRunning(false)
    }, 1000)
  }

  const handleComplete = () => {
    if (!kataProgress?.completed) {
      completeKata(kata.id, kata.xpReward)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h3 className="font-semibold">Kata of the Day</h3>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-amber-500 text-amber-900">
            {kata.isDaily ? 'DAILY' : 'PRACTICE'}
          </span>
          {kataProgress?.completed && (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">
              <CheckCircle className="h-3 w-3" />
              Completed
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{kata.prompt}</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <span className={CATEGORY_COLORS[kata.category] + ' px-2 py-0.5 rounded'}>
            {kata.category}
          </span>
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700">
            {kata.language}
          </span>
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700">
            ~{kata.estimatedMinutes} min
          </span>
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700">
            +{kata.xpReward} XP
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Your Solution</label>
        <textarea
          value={code}
          onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full h-48 font-mono text-sm p-3 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background resize-none"
          placeholder={kata.starterCode || 'Write your solution here...'}
          spellCheck={false}
        />
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={runCode}
            disabled={running}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-primary text-primary-foreground text-sm font-medium hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {running ? (
              <>
                <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                Running...
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <RotateCw className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Run Tests
              </>
            )}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setCode(kata.starterCode || '')}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background text-sm font-medium hover:bg-accent"
          >
            Reset
          </button>
          {!kataProgress?.completed && (
            <button
              onClick={handleComplete}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-green-500 text-white text-sm font-medium hover:bg-green-600"
            >
              <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Mark Complete
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {output && (
        <div className="rounded-lg bg-muted p-3 font-mono text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap max-h-40 overflow-auto">
          {output}
        </div>
      )}

      {kata.solution && (
        <details className="group">
          <summary className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground cursor-pointer">
            <RotateCw className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-open:rotate-90" />
            View Solution
          </summary>
          <pre className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-muted p-3 overflow-auto">
            <code className="font-mono text-sm">{kata.solution}</code>
          </pre>
        </details>
      )}

      {kata.testCases.length > 0 && (
        <details className="group">
          <summary className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground cursor-pointer">
            <RotateCw className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-open:rotate-90" />
            Test Cases
          </summary>
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
            {kata.testCases.map((tc, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="rounded-lg border border-input p-3 text-sm">
                <p className="font-medium">Test {i + 1}: {tc.description}</p>
                <p className="font-mono text-muted-foreground">Input: {tc.input}</p>
                <p className="font-mono text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">Expected: {tc.expected}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </details>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function ProjectList() {
  const { practice, completeProject } = useProgress()
  const projects = getAllMicroProjects()

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h3 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
        <FolderOpen className="h-5 w-5 text-green-500" />
        Micro Projects
      </h3>
      {projects.map((project) => {
        const progress = practice.projects[project.id]
        return (
          <div
            key={project.id}
            className="rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-card p-4 hover:border-border hover:shadow-sm transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h4 className="font-medium">{project.title}</h4>
                  {progress?.completed && (
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">
                      <CheckCircle className="h-3 w-3" />
                      Completed
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{project.description}</p>
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <span className={CATEGORY_COLORS[project.category] + ' px-2 py-0.5 rounded'}>
                    {project.category}
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700">
                    ~{project.estimatedMinutes} min
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700">
                    +{project.xpReward} XP
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 capitalize">
                    {project.difficulty}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-3 space-y-1">
                  <p className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">Requirements:</p>
                  <ul className="list-disc list-inside text-xs text-muted-foreground space-y-0.5">
                    {project.requirements.slice(0, 3).map((req, i) => (
                      <li key={i}>{req}</li>
                    ))}
                    {project.requirements.length > 3 && (
                      <li>+{project.requirements.length - 3} more...</li>
                    )}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 shrink-0">
                {!progress?.completed && (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => completeProject(project.id, project.xpReward)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-primary text-primary-foreground text-sm font-medium hover:bg-primary/90"
                  >
                    <Trophy className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                    Submit
                  </button>
                )}
                {progress?.completed && (
                  <button
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background text-sm font-medium hover:bg-accent"
                    disabled
                  >
                    <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 text-green-500" />
                    Submitted
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

function DrillList() {
  const { practice, completeDrill } = useProgress()
  const drills = getAllInterviewDrills()

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h3 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
        <Mic className="h-5 w-5 text-violet-500" />
        Interview Drills
      </h3>
      {drills.map((drill) => {
        const progress = practice.drills[drill.id]
        return (
          <div
            key={drill.id}
            className="rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-card p-4 hover:border-border hover:shadow-sm transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h4 className="font-medium">{drill.title}</h4>
                  {progress?.completed && (
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">
                      <CheckCircle className="h-3 w-3" />
                      Completed
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{drill.question}</p>
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  <span className={CATEGORY_COLORS[drill.category] + ' px-2 py-0.5 rounded'}>
                    {drill.category}
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 capitalize">
                    {drill.drillType}
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700">
                    ~{drill.estimatedMinutes} min
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700">
                    +{drill.xpReward} XP
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 capitalize">
                    {drill.difficulty}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {drill.followUps.length > 0 && (
                  <details className="mt-3 group">
                    <summary className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1">
                      <RotateCw className="h-3 w-3 transition-transform group-open:rotate-90" />
                      Follow-up questions
                    </summary>
                    <ul className="mt-2 list-disc list-inside text-xs text-muted-foreground space-y-1">
                      {drill.followUps.map((q, i) => (
                        <li key={i}>{q}</li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </details>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 shrink-0">
                {!progress?.completed && (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => completeDrill(drill.id, drill.xpReward)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-primary text-primary-foreground text-sm font-medium hover:bg-primary/90"
                  >
                    <Trophy className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                    Complete
                  </button>
                )}
                {progress?.completed && (
                  <button
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-input bg-background text-sm font-medium hover:bg-accent"
                    disabled
                  >
                    <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 text-green-500" />
                    Done
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

function PracticeDashboard() {
  const { xp, streak, practice, getPracticeStats } = useProgress()
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'flashcards' | 'katas' | 'projects' | 'drills' | 'all'>('flashcards')
  const [allItems, setAllItems] = useState<PracticeItem[]>([])
  const stats = getPracticeStats()

  useEffect(() => {
    setAllItems([
      ...getAllFlashcardsByCategory('javascript'),
      ...getAllFlashcardsByCategory('react'),
      ...getAllFlashcardsByCategory('nodejs'),
      ...getAllFlashcardsByCategory('first-client'),
    ])
  }, [])

  const filteredItems = allItems.filter((item) => {
    if (activeTab === 'all') return true
    const typeMap = {
      flashcards: 'flashcard',
      katas: 'kata',
      projects: 'project',
      drills: 'interview',
    }
    return item.type === typeMap[activeTab]
  })

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Stats Overview */}
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5">
        <StatCard
          title="Total XP"
          value={xp}
          icon={Zap}
          color="bg-amber-500"
          description={`Level ${Math.floor(xp / 100) + 1}`}
        />
        <StatCard
          title="Lesson Streak"
          value={streak.current}
          icon={Flame}
          color="bg-orange-500"
          description="days in a row"
        />
        <StatCard
          title="Practice Streak"
          value={practice.streak.current}
          icon={Trophy}
          color="bg-purple-500"
          description="practice days in a row"
        />
        <StatCard
          title="Flashcards Reviewed"
          value={stats.flashcardsReviewed}
          icon={Brain}
          color="bg-blue-500"
          description="cards in SRS"
        />
        <StatCard
          title="Katas Solved"
          value={stats.katasCompleted}
          icon={Code2}
          color="bg-green-500"
          description="code katas completed"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Main Practice Area */}
      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        {/* Left: Flashcards */}
        <section className="lg:col-span-1">
          <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-semibold">
                <BookOpen className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-500" />
                Flashcards Due
              </h2>
            </div>
            <FlashcardReview />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Right: Kata of the Day */}
        <section className="lg:col-span-1">
          <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 text-lg font-semibold">
              <Code2 className="h-5 w-5 text-green-500" />
              Kata of the Day
            </h2>
            <KataOfTheDay />
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* Projects & Drills */}
      <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
        <section>
          <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm">
            <ProjectList />
          </div>
        </section>
        <section>
          <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm">
            <DrillList />
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function StatCard({ title, value, icon: Icon, color, description }: {
  title: string
  value: number
  icon: React.ElementType
  color: string
  description: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{title}</p>
          <p className="text-3xl font-bold">{value}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>
        </div>
        <div className={`p-3 rounded-xl ${color} text-white`}>
          <Icon className="h-6 w-6" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function PracticePage() {
  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto py-8 px-4 max-w-6xl">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Practice Dashboard</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">
          Spaced repetition, daily katas, and interview prep — all in one place.
        </p>
      </div>

      <PracticeDashboard />
    </div>
  )
}

@media print {
  :root {
    color-scheme: light;
  }

  html,
  body {
    background: #fff !important;
    color: #000 !important;
  }

  body {
    font-size: 11pt;
  }

  * {
    box-shadow: none !important;
    text-shadow: none !important;
  }

  /* Cards, callouts, badges, and gradients should not carry dark-mode or
     accent backgrounds into print. Keep code blocks readable separately. */
  body *:not(pre):not(code) {
    background-color: transparent !important;
    color: #000 !important;
    border-color: #bbb !important;
  }

  [data-print-hide],
  [data-site-header],
  [data-site-footer],
  .fixed,
  iframe,
  button,
  input,
  textarea,
  select,
  form,
  nav {
    display: none !important;
  }

  main {
    display: block !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    margin: 0 !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
  }

  main > div,
  .prose {
    max-width: none !important;
  }

  a {
    color: #000 !important;
    text-decoration: underline;
  }

  h1,
  h2,
  h3,
  h4 {
    break-after: avoid;
  }

  p,
  li,
  blockquote {
    orphans: 3;
    widows: 3;
  }

  pre,
  blockquote,
  table,
  li {
    break-inside: avoid;
  }

  pre {
    white-space: pre-wrap !important;
    word-break: break-word;

===== FILE: src/app/privacy/page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import { LegalPage } from '@/components/legal-page'
import { NEWSLETTER_ENABLED } from '@/lib/newsletter-feature'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'

export const metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Privacy Policy',
  description:
    'What Nyxora Sync stores, where it lives, why it exists, and how to remove it. Local-first progress, optional accounts, no ads or trackers.',
  path: '/privacy',
})

export default function PrivacyPage() {
  return (
    <LegalPage
      badge="Legal"
      title="Privacy Policy"
      intro="Learning does not require an account. This page explains the data that does exist, why, and how to get rid of it."
      updated="October 23, 2026"
    >
      <h2>The short version</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>Progress and notes stay in your browser unless you sign in.</li>
        <li>An account stores your identity from GitHub, Google, or email, plus synced progress.</li>
        <li>Completion certificates are stored only when you sign in and finish a track.</li>
        <li>Comments, projects, and profile fields you publish are public.</li>
        <li>We do not sell data, run advertising, or use third-party tracking scripts.</li>
      </ul>

      <h2>What stays in your browser</h2>
      <p>
        Lesson completion, XP, streaks, bookmarks, notes, your last visited lesson, and your theme
        preference are stored in <code>localStorage</code>. They are not sent to a server while you
        are signed out. You can clear them at any time through your browser&apos;s site-data
        settings; doing so removes your local progress and notes.
      </p>

      <h2>What we store when you sign in</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>
          <strong>Account identity:</strong> email address, display name, profile image URL, and the
          provider account id from GitHub or Google. If you use an email sign-in link, we store the
          email address.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Session data:</strong> a session record with an expiry date so you stay signed in
          until you sign out or the session expires.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Synced progress:</strong> lesson completion, XP, streak, bookmarks, notes, and your
          last visited lesson. Sync happens only after you sign in.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Certificates:</strong> the track name, your display name, the issue date, and a
          random verification ID for each completed track. Certificates are issued automatically
          when every lesson in a track is complete.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Preferences:</strong> theme, preferred track, whether your progress is visible to
          others
          {NEWSLETTER_ENABLED
            ? ', and whether you opted into email updates.'
            : ', and any legacy email-updates preference saved before the newsletter was paused.'}
        </li>
      </ul>
      <p>
        We do not store passwords. Authentication is handled by OAuth providers or a single-use
        email link.
      </p>

      <h2>What is public</h2>
      <p>
        Lesson comments, project showcase entries, your display name, profile image, bio, and the
        profile links you choose to add are visible to anyone. If you turn off{' '}
        <strong>Show my learning progress</strong> in <Link href="/settings">settings</Link>, your XP,
        completed lessons, streaks, and badges are hidden from other people. Your email address is
        never shown on a public profile.
      </p>
      <p>
        Completion certificates are not shown on your public profile. Each certificate has a
        verification page that shows your display name, track name, and issue date to anyone who has
        the link. Search engines are asked not to index these pages, but the link itself is not
        secret. Share it only with people you want to see that information. The certificate is an
        informal, non-accredited record for showcase only, not an official qualification.
      </p>
      <p>
        Comments and projects can be deleted by their author. Deleting a parent comment also removes
        its replies.
      </p>

      {NEWSLETTER_ENABLED ? (
        <>
          <h2>Newsletter</h2>
          <p>
            If you subscribe, we store your email address, where it was collected (the footer or the
            newsletter page), the signup date, and when a confirmation email was sent. We send one
            confirmation email; only confirmed addresses receive updates. Confirmation and unsubscribe
            links are signed and expire, and unsubscribe links appear in every newsletter. Unsubscribing
            on the <Link href="/newsletter">newsletter page</Link> or through an email link deletes the
            stored address. For each broadcast we keep an audit record containing the sender, subject,
            recipient count, failure count, and date — not the email body. A global rate limit protects
            the signup form from abuse.
          </p>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <h2>Newsletter (paused)</h2>
          <p>
            Newsletter signup and delivery are currently disabled. Any address submitted before the
            newsletter was paused remains in the database but will not receive emails. To remove it,
            use the <Link href="/contact">contact form</Link>.
          </p>
        </>
      )}

      <h2>Contact messages</h2>
      <p>
        The <Link href="/contact">contact form</Link> stores your optional name, email address, topic,
        message, and the date. Messages are used only to read and respond to you. If the mail server
        is configured, a notification is emailed to the maintainers; a copy of the message remains
        in the database until it is resolved or deleted.
      </p>

      <h2>Cookies and local storage</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>
          <strong>Essential cookies:</strong> the NextAuth session cookie (when signed in) and a
          short-lived CSRF cookie during sign-in. These are required for authentication.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Local storage:</strong> theme, progress, notes, bookmarks, and whether you dismissed
          the cookie notice. These stay on your device.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>No tracking cookies:</strong> this site does not include advertising pixels,
          third-party analytics, or session-replay scripts.
        </li>
      </ul>
      <p>
        Outbound links may include UTM campaign parameters that identify Nyxora Sync as the referrer.
        Those parameters are added to the destination URL; they do not set a cookie on this site or
        track you across websites.
      </p>

      <h2>Service providers</h2>
      <p>These providers process data on our behalf so the site works:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>
          <strong>Vercel</strong> hosts the site and keeps operational logs that may include IP
          addresses and user agents for security and diagnostics.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Neon</strong> hosts the PostgreSQL database that contains accounts, progress,
          comments, projects, newsletter subscribers, and contact messages.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>GitHub and Google</strong> handle OAuth sign-in when you choose those providers.
        </li>
        <li>
          <strong>Our email provider</strong> delivers sign-in links and, when configured, contact
          notifications.
          {NEWSLETTER_ENABLED
            ? ' It also delivers newsletter confirmation and broadcast emails.'
            : ' Newsletter delivery is currently disabled.'}
        </li>
      </ul>
      <p>
        These providers may process data in countries other than yours. Their own privacy policies
        and security terms apply to their processing.
      </p>

      <h2>What we do not do</h2>
      <p>
        We do not sell personal data, use it for advertising, build advertising profiles, or send
        your notes and private messages to third parties. Public content is public because you chose
        to publish it, not because it was harvested.
      </p>

      <h2>Retention</h2>
      <ul>
        <li>Account data, including issued certificates, remains until you request deletion.</li>
        <li>Comments and projects remain until you delete them or your account is deleted.</li>
        {NEWSLETTER_ENABLED ? (
          <li>
            Newsletter addresses remain until you unsubscribe, including unconfirmed addresses that
            never completed the double opt-in step.
          </li>
        ) : (
          <li>
            Newsletter addresses collected before the newsletter was paused remain stored until you
            request deletion through the contact form.
          </li>
        )}
        <li>Contact messages remain until they are resolved or deleted.</li>
        <li>Hosting logs are retained according to the hosting provider&apos;s policy.</li>
      </ul>

      <h2>Your choices and rights</h2>
      <p>
        Depending on where you live, you may have the right to access, correct, export, or delete
        your personal data, and to object to or restrict certain processing. You can:
      </p>
      <ul>
        <li>Edit your profile and hide your progress in <Link href="/settings">settings</Link>.</li>
        <li>
          View your certificates in <Link href="/certificates">your certificate list</Link>; deleting
          your account also deletes the certificate records.
        </li>
        <li>Delete your comments and projects directly on the site.</li>
        {NEWSLETTER_ENABLED && (
          <li>
            Unsubscribe from the newsletter on the <Link href="/newsletter">newsletter page</Link> or
            through the link in any newsletter email.
          </li>
        )}
        <li>
          Request an account export or deletion through the <Link href="/contact">contact form</Link>.
          We may ask you to verify control of the account first.
        </li>
        <li>Clear local progress and notes through your browser.</li>
      </ul>
      <p>
        Nothing here limits rights you have under mandatory local law, including the right to complain
        to your data protection authority.
      </p>

      <h2>Security</h2>
      <p>
        The site is served over HTTPS. Database access is restricted to the application, and
        authentication uses OAuth or single-use email links rather than stored passwords. No system
        is perfectly secure; if you find a vulnerability, please report it privately through the{' '}
        <Link href="/contact">contact page</Link> instead of posting details publicly.
      </p>

      <h2>Children</h2>
      <p>
        The site is intended for learners aged 13 and older, or 16 and older where local law sets a
        higher age for digital consent. It is not directed at younger children. If we learn that a
        child below the applicable age created an account, we will delete it.
      </p>

      <h2>Changes to this policy</h2>
      <p>
        If the data practices change, this page will be updated and the date at the top will change.
        Material changes that affect existing accounts will be announced on the site before they take
        effect.
      </p>

      <h2>Contact</h2>
      <p>
        Privacy questions and requests go through the <Link href="/contact">contact form</Link>,
        choosing the &ldquo;Privacy or account request&rdquo; topic.
      </p>
    </LegalPage>
  )
}

const developerRoadmap = beginnerRoadmap.slice(1).map((phase, i) => ({
  ...phase,
  weeks: ['Weeks 1-3', 'Weeks 4-5', 'Weeks 6-8', 'Weeks 9-10'][i],
}))

const specializationTrack = getTrack('specializations')

const specializationIcons: Record<string, typeof Atom> = {
  Atom,
  Cloud,
  Boxes,
  Smartphone,
}

const languageTracks = tracks.filter(
  (track) => track.category === 'programming-languages'
)

const languageIcons: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  c: FileCode,
  cpp: Braces,
  csharp: Hash,
  python: Terminal,
}

function LanguageRoadmap({
  track,
  index,
}: {
  track: (typeof languageTracks)[number]
  index: number
}) {
  const Icon = languageIcons[track.key] ?? FileCode
  const lessonCount = track.modules.reduce(
    (sum, module) => sum + module.lessons.length,
    0
  )
  const hours = track.modules.reduce((sum, module) => sum + module.estimatedHours, 0)

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-4 rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-5">
        <div
          className={cn(
            'flex h-12 w-12 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl',
            getAccent(track.color).icon
          )}
        >
          <Icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">{track.name}</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{track.description}</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {track.modules.length} levels · {lessonCount} lessons · ~{hours} hours
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {track.modules.map((module, moduleIndex) => (
        <Reveal key={module.id} delay={Math.min((index + moduleIndex) * 0.05, 0.2)}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="pt-6">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted text-sm font-semibold">
                  {moduleIndex + 1}
                </span>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-semibold">{module.name}</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {module.lessons.length} lessons · ~{module.estimatedHours} hours
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{module.description}</p>
              <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {module.lessons.map((lesson) => (
                  <Link
                    key={lesson.id}
                    href={`/learn/${track.key}/${module.key}/${lesson.id}`}
                    className="group flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/50 px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
                  >
                    <span className="flex h-5 w-5 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-background text-[10px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground">
                      {lesson.order}
                    </span>
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{lesson.title}</span>
                    <ArrowRight
                      className="h-3.5 w-3.5 flex-shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-foreground"
                      aria-hidden="true"
                    />
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Reveal>
      ))}

      <div className="pt-2 text-center">
        <Button asChild variant="outline" className="gap-2">
          <Link href={`/learn/${track.key}`}>
            Start the {track.name} path
            <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function RoadmapPhase({ phase, index, color }: { phase: typeof beginnerRoadmap[0]; index: number; color: string }) {
  const Icon = phase.icon
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      {/* Connector line */}
      {index > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute left-7 top-0 -translate-y-full h-8 w-px bg-border" aria-hidden="true" />
      )}
      <Card className="overflow-hidden">
        <CardContent className="pt-6">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div
              className={cn('flex h-14 w-14 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl', getAccent(color).icon)}
            >
              <Icon className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold">{phase.phase}</h3>
                <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">{phase.weeks}</Badge>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {phase.steps.map((step, si) => (
                  <div
                    key={si}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/50 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  >
                    <step.icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span className="truncate">{step.name}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  )
}

  title: 'Full Learning Roadmap',

})

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">

              <span className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 via-violet-500 to-amber-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent">

              </span>
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto text-lg text-muted-foreground">
              Complete, structured paths from absolute beginner to job-ready developer — for the web
              and for the languages behind it.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="py-12 sm:py-16">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <Tabs defaultValue="beginner">
              <TabsList className="grid w-full max-w-md mx-auto grid-cols-2 mb-10">

              <TabsContent value="beginner" className="space-y-6">
                <div className="text-center mb-8">

                    16 weeks · No prior experience needed · ~110 hours of learning
                  </p>
                </div>
                {beginnerRoadmap.map((phase, i) => (

                    <Link href="/learn/beginner">
                      Start Beginner Track <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </Link>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </TabsContent>

              <TabsContent value="developer" className="space-y-6">
                <div className="text-center mb-8">
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground">
                    10 weeks · Requires JS/ES6+, Git, and basic terminal knowledge · ~65 hours of learning
                  </p>
                </div>
                {developerRoadmap.map((phase, i) => (
                  <Reveal key={phase.phase} delay={Math.min(i * 0.06, 0.24)}>
                    <RoadmapPhase phase={phase} index={i} color="violet" />
                  </Reveal>
                ))}
                <div className="pt-6 text-center">
                  <Button asChild size="lg" className="gap-2">
                    <Link href="/learn/developer">
                      Start Developer Track <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </Link>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </TabsContent>
            </Tabs>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Programming Languages */}
        {languageTracks.length > 0 && (
          <section className="border-y border-border/50 py-12 sm:py-16">
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
              <div className="mb-10 text-center">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
                  Programming Languages
                </p>
                <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                  Choose a language path
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-3 mx-auto max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">
                  Each path has three levels and twelve written lessons. Every level ends with a
                  project; every path ends with a capstone.
                </p>
              </div>
              <Tabs defaultValue={languageTracks[0].key}>
                <TabsList className="mx-auto mb-10 grid w-full max-w-2xl grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
                  {languageTracks.map((track) => {
                    const Icon = languageIcons[track.key] ?? FileCode
                    return (
                      <TabsTrigger key={track.key} value={track.key} className="gap-2">
                        <Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {track.name}
                      </TabsTrigger>
                    )
                  })}
                </TabsList>
                {languageTracks.map((track, index) => (
                  <TabsContent key={track.key} value={track.key}>
                    <LanguageRoadmap track={track} index={index} />
                  </TabsContent>
                ))}
              </Tabs>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Specializations */}
        <section className="py-12 sm:py-16 bg-muted/30">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="text-center mb-12">
              <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">After MERN: Specialize</h2>
              <p className="mt-3 text-muted-foreground max-w-2xl mx-auto">
                Finished the core MERN curriculum? Pick a complete specialization module and keep going.
              </p>
            </div>
            {specializationTrack && (
              <>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
                  {specializationTrack.modules.map((spec) => {
                    const Icon = specializationIcons[spec.icon] ?? Atom
                    return (
                      <Link
                        key={spec.id}
                        href={`/learn/${specializationTrack.key}/${spec.key}`}
                        className="group block"
                      >
                        <Card className="h-full hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                          <CardContent className="pt-6 flex items-start gap-4">
                            <div className={cn('flex h-12 w-12 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl', getAccent(spec.color).icon)}>
                              <Icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="min-w-0">
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                                <h3 className="font-semibold group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">{spec.name}</h3>
                                <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">
                                  {spec.lessons.length} lessons
                                </Badge>
                              </div>
                              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">{spec.description}</p>
                              <p className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-primary">
                                Start specialization
                                <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                              </p>
                            </div>
                          </CardContent>
                        </Card>
                      </Link>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>
                <div className="mt-8 text-center">
                  <Button asChild variant="outline" className="gap-2">
                    <Link href={`/learn/${specializationTrack.key}`}>
                      Explore all specializations
                      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </Link>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/robots.ts =====
import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next'
import { absoluteUrl, siteUrl } from '@/lib/seo'

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  return {
    rules: [
      {
        userAgent: '*',
        allow: '/',
        disallow: [
          '/api/',
          '/settings',
          '/dashboard',
          '/auth/',
          '/admin/',
          '/search',
          '/newsletter/confirm',
          '/newsletter/unsubscribe',
        ],
      },
    ],
    sitemap: absoluteUrl('/sitemap.xml'),
    host: siteUrl,
  }
}

===== FILE: src/app/search/page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link'
import Form from 'next/form'
import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import {
  BookOpen,
  ExternalLink,
  FileText,
  FolderTree,
  HelpCircle,
  Library,
  Rocket,
  Route,
  Search,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/components/header'
import { Footer } from '@/components/footer'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { groupSearchResults, searchSite, type SearchResult, type SearchResultType } from '@/lib/search'
import { pageMetadata } from '@/lib/seo'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

export const metadata: Metadata = pageMetadata({
  title: 'Search',
  description: 'Search lessons, modules, resources, pages, FAQ answers, and community projects on Nyxora Sync.',
  path: '/search',
  noIndex: true,
})

const typeIcons: Record<SearchResultType, typeof BookOpen> = {
  lesson: BookOpen,
  module: FolderTree,
  track: Route,
  page: FileText,
  faq: HelpCircle,
  resource: Library,
  project: Rocket,
}

const typeColors: Record<SearchResultType, string> = {
  lesson: 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400',
  module: 'bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400',
  track: 'bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400',
  page: 'bg-slate-500/15 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400',
  faq: 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400',
  resource: 'bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400',
  project: 'bg-indigo-500/15 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400',
}

const typeLabels: Record<SearchResultType, string> = {
  lesson: 'Lesson',
  module: 'Module',
  track: 'Track',
  page: 'Page',
  faq: 'FAQ',
  resource: 'Resource',
  project: 'Project',
}

const suggestions = [
  {
    title: 'Start with Web Foundations',
    description: 'New to HTML, CSS, and JavaScript? Begin at the beginning.',
    href: '/learn/beginner',
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    title: 'Jump into the MERN stack',
    description: 'Already know JavaScript? Go straight to Node, Express, MongoDB, and React.',
    href: '/learn/developer',
    icon: Route,
  },
  {
    title: 'Find a free resource',
    description: 'Search the directory of videos, docs, courses, books, and tools.',
    href: '/resources',
    icon: Library,
  },
  {
    title: 'Browse learner projects',
    description: 'See what other people built and leave useful feedback.',
    href: '/community/showcase',
    icon: Rocket,
  },
]

function ResultCard({ result, typeLabel }: { result: SearchResult; typeLabel: string }) {
  const Icon = typeIcons[result.type]

  const content = (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div
          className={cn(
            'flex h-10 w-10 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl',
            typeColors[result.type]
          )}
        >
          <Icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide">
              {typeLabel}
            </Badge>
            {result.meta && (
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{result.meta}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <h3 className="mt-2 font-semibold leading-snug transition-colors group-hover:text-primary">
            {result.title}
          </h3>
          <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{result.description}</p>
        </div>
        <ExternalLink
          className={cn(
            'mt-1 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0 text-muted-foreground/50 transition-colors group-hover:text-foreground',
            result.external ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0'
          )}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
      </div>
    </>
  )

  const className =
    'group block rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-4 transition-all hover:border-border hover:shadow-md motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-0.5 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2'

  if (result.external) {
    return (
      <a
        href={result.href}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        className={className}
      >
        {content}
      </a>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Link href={result.href} className={className}>
      {content}
    </Link>
  )
}

export default async function SearchPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ q?: string | string[] }>
}) {
  const params = await searchParams
  const rawQuery = params.q
  const query = (Array.isArray(rawQuery) ? rawQuery[0] : rawQuery)?.trim().slice(0, 120) ?? ''
  const results = query ? await searchSite(query) : []
  const groups = groupSearchResults(results)

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <Header />
      <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1} className="flex-1">
        <section className="border-b border-border/50 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500/5 via-background to-violet-500/5 py-12 sm:py-16">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">
              <Search className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              Site search
            </div>
            <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
              Find the lesson, resource, or answer you need.
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-lg text-muted-foreground">
              Search across lessons, modules, tracks, the free resource directory, public pages,
              FAQ answers, and community projects.
            </p>

            <Form action="/search" role="search" className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <Search
                  className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
                <Input
                  type="search"
                  name="q"
                  defaultValue={query}
                  placeholder="Try “React hooks”, “MongoDB”, or “accessibility”..."
                  className="h-11 pl-10"
                  aria-label="Search the site"
                  autoComplete="off"
                />
              </div>
              <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="h-11 gap-2">
                <Search className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                Search
              </Button>
            </Form>
          </div>
        </section>

        <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 sm:py-12 lg:px-8">
          {query ? (
            <>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground" aria-live="polite">
                  {results.length} {results.length === 1 ? 'result' : 'results'} for{' '}
                  <span className="font-medium text-foreground">“{query}”</span>
                </p>
                <Link
                  href="/search"
                  className="text-sm font-medium text-emerald-700 underline-offset-4 hover:underline dark:text-emerald-400"
                >
                  Clear search
                </Link>
              </div>

              {results.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-border/60 bg-muted/20 p-8 text-center sm:p-12">
                  <Sparkles className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground/60" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold">No matches yet</h2>
                  <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-lg text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    Try a shorter phrase, a technology name, or a topic like “forms”, “deployment”,
                    or “testing”. You can also browse the curriculum or ask in the community.
                  </p>
                  <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
                    <Button asChild>
                      <Link href="/learn">Browse lessons</Link>
                    </Button>
                    <Button asChild variant="outline">
                      <Link href="/contact">Ask for help</Link>
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-8 space-y-10">
                  {groups.map((group) => (
                    <section key={group.type} aria-labelledby={`search-${group.type}`}>
                      <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <h2 id={`search-${group.type}`} className="text-lg font-semibold">
                          {group.label}
                        </h2>
                        <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
                          {group.results.length}
                        </Badge>
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-3">
                        {group.results.map((result) => (
                          <ResultCard key={result.id} result={result} typeLabel={typeLabels[result.type]} />
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </section>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          ) : (
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                Not sure where to start?
              </div>
              <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Pick a path</h2>
              <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {suggestions.map((suggestion) => (
                  <Link
                    key={suggestion.href}
                    href={suggestion.href}
                    className="group rounded-xl border border-border/50 bg-card p-5 transition-all hover:border-border hover:shadow-md motion-safe:hover:-translate-y-0.5 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
                  >
                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                      <suggestion.icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="mt-4 font-semibold group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                      {suggestion.title}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{suggestion.description}</p>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/app/sitemap.ts =====
import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next'
import { tracks, trackCategories } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { NEWSLETTER_ENABLED } from '@/lib/newsletter-feature'
import { absoluteUrl, canonicalLearnPath } from '@/lib/seo'

type ChangeFrequency = NonNullable<MetadataRoute.Sitemap[number]['changeFrequency']>

const staticPages: Array<{
  path: string
  priority: number
  changeFrequency: ChangeFrequency
}> = [
  { path: '/', priority: 1, changeFrequency: 'weekly' },
  { path: '/learn', priority: 0.9, changeFrequency: 'weekly' },
  { path: '/learn/languages', priority: 0.9, changeFrequency: 'weekly' },
  { path: '/categories', priority: 0.9, changeFrequency: 'weekly' },
  { path: '/roadmap', priority: 0.8, changeFrequency: 'monthly' },
  { path: '/resources', priority: 0.7, changeFrequency: 'monthly' },
  { path: '/playground', priority: 0.6, changeFrequency: 'monthly' },
  { path: '/community', priority: 0.6, changeFrequency: 'weekly' },
  { path: '/community/showcase', priority: 0.6, changeFrequency: 'weekly' },
  { path: '/contribute', priority: 0.5, changeFrequency: 'monthly' },
  { path: '/about', priority: 0.5, changeFrequency: 'yearly' },
  { path: '/faq', priority: 0.5, changeFrequency: 'monthly' },
  { path: '/contact', priority: 0.4, changeFrequency: 'yearly' },
  { path: '/code-of-conduct', priority: 0.3, changeFrequency: 'yearly' },
  { path: '/privacy', priority: 0.3, changeFrequency: 'yearly' },
  { path: '/terms', priority: 0.3, changeFrequency: 'yearly' },
  { path: '/accessibility', priority: 0.3, changeFrequency: 'yearly' },
]

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  const entries = new Map<string, MetadataRoute.Sitemap[number]>()

  function add(path: string, priority: number, changeFrequency: ChangeFrequency) {
    if (entries.has(path)) return
    entries.set(path, {
      url: absoluteUrl(path),
      changeFrequency,
      priority,
    })
  }

  for (const page of staticPages) {
    add(page.path, page.priority, page.changeFrequency)
  }

  for (const category of trackCategories) {
    if (tracks.some((track) => track.category === category.id)) {
      add(`/categories/${category.id}`, 0.8, 'weekly')
    }
  }

  if (NEWSLETTER_ENABLED) {
    add('/newsletter', 0.4, 'yearly')
  }

  for (const track of tracks) {
    add(`/learn/${track.key}`, 0.8, 'weekly')

    for (const mod of track.modules) {
      add(canonicalLearnPath(track.key, mod.key), 0.7, 'monthly')

      for (const lesson of mod.lessons) {
        add(canonicalLearnPath(track.key, mod.key, lesson.id), 0.6, 'monthly')
      }
    }
  }

  return [...entries.values()]
}

  description:
    'The plain-language terms for using Nyxora Sync: accounts, acceptable use, content licenses, moderation, and the limits of a free educational site.',
  path: '/terms',

export default function TermsPage() {

    <LegalPage
      badge="Legal"
      title="Terms of Service"
      intro="These are the rules for using Nyxora Sync. They are written to be read, not to hide behind."
      updated="October 23, 2026"
    >

        <Link href="/privacy">privacy policy</Link>. If you do not agree, use the site without an
